- Idea: Clothing becomes animal skin, giving the human body a fur that reacts to threat and affection.
- What it is: An emotive garment covered in a fur of fibre optics that bristles when onlookers look angry or surprised and purrs when stroked, as dogs, cats and mice raise their fur.
- How it works: Fibre optics embedded in silicone are moved by a pneumatic soft-robotic system, driven by a camera that classifies onlookers' facial expressions.
- Video: https://vimeo.com/232258166
- Images: https://behnazfarahi.com/opale/1.jpg
- Project page: http://behnazfarahi.com/opale/

#### Art for Dogs — Dominic Wilcox (2016)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Smell & taste, Altered vision · Medium: Multisensory installation
- Idea: The gallery is designed around a dog's sensory priorities: smell first, blue-yellow colour vision, low eye level and play.
- What it is: An exhibition of interactive sculptures designed for dogs: a giant food bowl filled with ball-pit balls, a car-window simulator with scents blowing past and a moving landscape, and a fountain of water leaping between bowls.
- How it works: Mechanical sculptures with scent fans, ball pits and water jets, coloured for dichromatic canine vision and built at dog height.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=h7hpI0OodAY
- Project page: https://www.dominicwilcox.com/

#### Experiencing Nature: Embodying a Cow and a Coral — Stanford Virtual Human Interaction Lab (2016)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement, Touch & haptics · Medium: VR headset
- Idea: Being the animal, not watching it, raised feelings of connection with nature and concern that lasted a week.
- What it is: Three experiments in which participants became a cow walking to a slaughterhouse or a coral on an acidifying reef, on all fours with a haptic prod, compared with watching the same on video.
- How it works: Head-mounted display with full-body tracking; participants crawl on hands and knees while a stick touches them in sync with the virtual cattle prod.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1111/jcc4.12173 (Journal of Computer-Mediated Communication 2016)
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aQke1eQHSAA

#### GoatMan — Thomas Thwaites (2016)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement, Smell & taste · Medium: Wearable & sensory device, Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Ig Nobel Prize in Biology 2016; Nature–Design Triennial, Cooper Hewitt 2019
- Idea: Becoming animal hits the body's limits at once: pain, cold, gait and digestion decide how far you get.
- What it is: The designer built prosthetic legs, a helmet and an attempt at an artificial rumen, then crossed the Swiss Alps on all fours with a herd of goats for several days.
- How it works: Custom front and hind leg prostheses made with prosthetist Glyn Heath, a protective helmet, and consultation with neuroscientists and goat behaviour experts.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-IPub-Fipz8
- Images: https://www.thomasthwaites.com/folio5/wp-content/uploads/2016/03/Goat8-Thomas_Thwaites-photo-Tim_Bowditch.jpg https://www.thomasthwaites.com/folio5/wp-content/uploads/2016/03/Goat14-Thomas_Thwaites-photo-Tim_Bowditch.jpg
- Project page: https://www.thomasthwaites.com/a-holiday-from-being-human-goatman/

#### K-9_topology: Hybrid Family — Maja Smrekar (2016)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Breath & interoception, Body schema & movement · Medium: Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Berlin 2016; Prix Ars Electronica 2017 (Golden Nica, Hybrid Art, K-9_topology)
- Idea: Becoming-animal through hormones: prolactin and oxytocin make a cross-species family at the level of the body.
- What it is: After three months of seclusion with her dogs, the artist induced lactation through breast pumping and diet and breastfed a puppy named Ada in a public performance.
- How it works: Systematic breast pumping to stimulate the pituitary gland, a galactagogue diet, and a durational performance documented in photographs with Manuel Vason.
- Images: https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/5c326f997c9327edb6347bf8/1575463206163-6D6W7CEJ7DBLDNJJW29I/03_K9%2BHYBRID%2BFAMILY.jpg
- Project page: https://www.majasmrekar.org/k-9topology-hybrid-family

#### iAnimal — Animal Equality (2016)
- Type: Immersive film · Senses: Altered vision · Medium: 360° / immersive film
- Idea: A critical counterpoint: the 360° camera offers the animal's vantage point but not its senses, and relies on witness rather than embodiment.
- What it is: A series of 360° films shot inside farms and slaughterhouses at the height of the animals, pigs, chickens and dairy cows, so that viewers stand where the animals stand.
- How it works: 360° cameras placed at animal eye level in industrial farms, with celebrity narration and headset screenings in public spaces.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=53CAL47OWxo
- Project page: https://animalequality.org/

#### Catlateral Damage — Manekoware (2015)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, VR headset
- Idea: The first-person cat: paws in view and physics objects to swat turn a cat's mischief into the whole game.
- What it is: A first-person game in which you are a house cat trying to knock as many objects as possible onto the floor before time runs out.
- How it works: First-person paw swipes on physics-simulated household objects, with optional VR support on PC.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HWhL3Vd5u0s
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/329860/header.jpg
- Project page: http://www.catlateraldamage.com/

#### I Wear the Dog's Hair, and the Dog Wears My Hair — AKI INOMATA (2014)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Touch & haptics, Body schema & movement · Medium: Wearable & sensory device, Multisensory installation
- Shown at: HAGISO Tokyo 2014
- Idea: Swapping coats is a quiet form of becoming-with: each body wears the other's skin rather than imitating its behaviour.
- What it is: Over several years the artist collected her own hair and that of her dog Cielo, then made a cape of dog hair for herself and a cape of her hair for the dog, and filmed them wearing each other's coats.
- How it works: Hand-felted and woven capes from collected human and dog hair, shown as objects with a two-channel video installation.
- Video: https://vimeo.com/102524253
- Images: https://www.aki-inomata.com/shared/img/works/11/11-02.jpg https://www.aki-inomata.com/shared/img/works/11/11-03.jpg
- Project page: https://www.aki-inomata.com/works/dogs/

#### K-9_topology: Ecce Canis — Maja Smrekar (2014)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Smell & taste · Medium: Multisensory installation
- Shown at: Prix Ars Electronica 2017 (Golden Nica, Hybrid Art, K-9_topology)
- Idea: Human and dog meet in a shared molecule; the gallery is entered through smell, the dog's leading sense.
- What it is: Serotonin extracted from the blood of the artist and her dog Byron was turned into a scent that fills a spherical cave next to the laboratory machine that separated it.
- How it works: High-performance liquid chromatography separated serotonin fractions from platelet-rich plasma, which were then developed into an odour.
- Images: https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/5c326f997c9327edb6347bf8/1575227420980-BZIGFQFH9X8CSEG1TXDO/01_K-9_topology_ECCE+CANIS.jpg
- Project page: https://www.majasmrekar.org/ekce-canis

#### Second Livestock — Austin Stewart (2014)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset, Multisensory installation
- Idea: Putting the headset on the animal exposes VR's promise of freedom as a way of keeping bodies confined.
- What it is: A speculative project proposing VR headsets and omnidirectional treadmills for battery-farmed chickens, so that caged birds could roam a virtual free-range farm.
- How it works: Mock-ups of a chicken-sized headset and treadmill with a simple virtual pasture, presented as installation, website and video.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=oRFSsNegE24
- Project page: https://www.isea-symposium-archives.org/art-events/austin-stewart-second-livestock/

#### May the Horse Live in Me — Art Orienté Objet (2011)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Body schema & movement, Breath & interoception · Medium: Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Kapelica Gallery Ljubljana 2011; VIDA 14.0 (third prize) 2012
- Idea: Becoming-animal moves inside the body: horse immunoglobulins change the artist's sleep, fear and sense of self.
- What it is: Marion Laval-Jeantet was injected with horse blood plasma after months of immune preparation, then walked beside a horse on hoof-like stilts, communicating with it.
- How it works: Gradual exposure to horse immunoglobulins under medical supervision, a final plasma injection, and a public performance with custom stilt-hooves.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yx_E4DUWXbE
- Project page: https://www.paris-art.com/que-le-cheval-vive-en-moi/

#### necomimi — neurowear (2011)
- Type: Product · Senses: Body schema & movement, Breath & interoception · Medium: Wearable & sensory device
- Shown at: Tokyo Game Show 2011
- Idea: Wearing an animal's expressive organ turns hidden inner states into visible cat-like body language.
- What it is: Brainwave-controlled cat ears: a headband reads the wearer's EEG and makes the ears perk up when they concentrate, droop when they relax and wiggle when they are both.
- How it works: Single-electrode NeuroSky EEG sensor driving two servo-actuated ears.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_L_VkXbCIqY
- Images: https://i.ytimg.com/vi/_L_VkXbCIqY/hqdefault.jpg
- Project page: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_L_VkXbCIqY

#### Music for Dogs — Laurie Anderson (2010)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Hearing & vibration · Medium: Performance & participatory, Spatial audio
- Shown at: Vivid Sydney 2010; Times Square New York 2016
- Idea: Composing for another species' hearing means writing partly in frequencies the human audience cannot perceive.
- What it is: An outdoor concert composed for dogs, with sounds in the canine hearing range, performed on the forecourt of the Sydney Opera House for an audience of dogs and their owners.
- How it works: Live electronic and violin performance with high-frequency content above human hearing, amplified outdoors.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3bS2Kuhe6tk
- Project page: https://laurieanderson.com/

#### Dog's Life — Frontier Developments (2003)
- Type: Game · Senses: Smell & taste, Altered vision · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Canine smell made visible: the world desaturates and odours become coloured clouds to follow.
- What it is: An adventure in which you are Jake, a dog searching for a friend; 'Smellovision' switches to a dog's-eye view in which scents appear as coloured clouds.
- How it works: A first-person smell mode that drains colour from the scene and overlays coloured scent sources on PlayStation 2.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hShavuoq2vY
- Images: https://i.ytimg.com/vi/hShavuoq2vY/hqdefault.jpg
- Project page: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dog%27s_Life

#### Man-Dog performances — Oleg Kulik (1994)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Moscow 1994; Deitch Projects New York 1997
- Idea: Becoming dog as social provocation: the animal body exposes the violence and limits of the human community around it.
- What it is: A series of performances in which the artist, naked and on a leash, acted as a dog, barking and biting visitors, from Moscow in 1994 to 'I Bite America and America Bites Me' at Deitch Projects, New York, in 1997.
- How it works: Durational live performance with leash, cage and audience interaction, documented in photographs and video.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M2CZyLjtIVk
- Project page: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oleg_Kulik

### Wild mammals

Deer, foxes, wolves, elephants, bears, primates and other wild mammals.

#### Become the Beast — Perttu Hämäläinen (2026)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Multisensory installation
- Idea: A quadruped body can be driven by a human lying on their back, turning abdominal exercise into animal gait.
- What it is: An exergame in which the player lies on the floor and cycles arms and legs to run as a tiger.
- How it works: Overhead Kinect depth sensing of the supine player's limbs mapped to a tiger's four-legged locomotion on a screen.
- Paper: https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15428 (arXiv 2026)
- Images: https://arxiv.org/html/2603.15428v2/Source/Images/teaserFinal.png

#### Wolfborn — Shengdong Zhao (2026)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Hearing & vibration, Body schema & movement, Breath & interoception · Medium: VR headset
- Idea: Becoming an animal can lend us its voice: a howl we could not make as ourselves.
- What it is: A VR demo in which users embody a wolf and release stress by howling, without making any audible sound.
- How it works: Pseudo-vocalization combining breath or throat gestures with visual, auditory and tactile feedback of the wolf's howl.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3802974.3808033 (DIS 2026 Companion)

#### "I look like a gorilla, but don't move like one!" — Omar A. Khan (2025)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset
- Idea: An animal body feels more one's own when its gait matches its form.
- What it is: Participants moved as a human or a gorilla avatar using either human arm-swing or gorilla-like arm-roll locomotion.
- How it works: Head-mounted VR with controller-based arm-swing and arm-roll locomotion techniques in a 2×2 study.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1109/vrw66409.2025.00247 (IEEE VR 2025 Workshops)

#### I Am Monkey — New Folder Games (2025)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: The zoo gaze reversed: the player is the one on display, looking out through bars at people.
- What it is: A VR sandbox in which you are a monkey in a zoo cage; visitors pass by, some offering bananas, others teasing, and you can charm them or fight back.
- How it works: Arm-based climbing, grabbing and throwing with scripted visitor behaviour on Meta Quest and Steam.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LJCfvsjFybM
- Images: https://queststoredb.com/media/24775738222115749_cover_landscape.webp
- Project page: https://www.meta.com/experiences/i-am-monkey/24775738222115749/

#### ZooWear — Pingting Chen (2025)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Touch & haptics, Hearing & vibration · Medium: Wearable & sensory device
- Idea: Wearing an animal's ears lets you feel its place in the food chain.
- What it is: Head-mounted cartoon animal ears that vibrate with patterns simulating how an animal reacts to predators and prey while visitors walk through a zoo.
- How it works: Head-worn ears with vibrotactile actuators triggered by location in the zoo; field study with visitors.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1080/10447318.2025.2583471 (International Journal of Human–Computer Interaction 2025)

#### Pine Cone Prowl: Embodying a Flying Squirrel — Gamification Group, Tampere University (2024)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: Gliding, not flying: design the body around one small mammal's way of moving.
- What it is: A VR game in which the player is a flying squirrel gliding between trees in a Finnish forest.
- How it works: Head-mounted VR with arm gestures for spreading the gliding membrane (likely controller-based).
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3681716.3690625 (Academic Mindtrek 2024)

#### Playing for a Better Future: Animal Avatars and Attitudes — Andrey Krekhov (2024)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Playing as an animal can shift implicit attitudes towards wildlife, as avatar swaps do for human groups.
- What it is: A study (N=78) in which people played as a fox in Endling or a bee in Bee Simulator, then took implicit attitude tests about animals.
- How it works: Commercial games played on screen, followed by implicit association tests and questionnaires.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3677096 (Proceedings of the ACM on HCI (CHI PLAY) 2024)

#### Primate Visions: Macaque Macabre — Natasha Tontey (2024)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement, Multisensory · Medium: Multisensory installation, Screen & web, Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Museum MACAN, Jakarta 2024–25 (Audemars Piguet Contemporary); FID Marseille 2025; Singapore International Film Festival 2025; BFI London Film Festival 2025
- Idea: Becoming macaque is a ritual technology: ancestors, pests and endangered kin at once.
- What it is: An immersive exhibition and film in which a primatologist frees a troop of yaki (black-crested macaques) in North Sulawesi; the actors playing the macaques re-enact Mawolay, the Minahasan ritual in which shamans in monkey costumes become macaques to keep them from the villages.
- How it works: Film with costumed performers, campy 1990s Indonesian soap-opera aesthetics and a walk-in installation.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Z16Cf6jFbw
- Images: https://admin.fidmarseille.org/media/pages/festivals/festival-36/films/primate-visions-macaque-macabre/fdd5085136-1770734979/fidmarseille-2025-primate-visions-macaque-macabre-film-still-0-2567367-1200x630-crop.jpg
- Project page: https://www.museummacan.org/exhibition/macaque-macabre?lang=en

#### Endling - Extinction is Forever — Herobeat Studios (2022)
- Type: Game · Senses: Smell & taste, Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Extinction from inside a fox family: environmental damage is felt as hunger and danger for your cubs.
- What it is: An adventure in which you are the last mother fox on an Earth damaged by humans, foraging at night to feed four cubs while avoiding people and their machines.
- How it works: 2.5D side-on exploration with a day-night cycle and a scent-tracking sense that leaves visible trails.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kiM2_XB_HZE
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/898890/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.thqnordicmobile.com/en/games/endling/

#### AWAY: The Survival Series — Breaking Walls (2021)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: The documentary subject becomes the player: a narrator describes the animal the player is controlling.
- What it is: A survival adventure styled as a nature documentary, in which you are a sugar glider gliding between trees and fending off predators to protect your family.
- How it works: Third-person gliding and climbing with documentary-style narration and cameras.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xGYbtvKcjmQ
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/750200/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.breakingwalls.co/

#### Bubalus Bubalis 16hz-40,000hz — Zheng Mahler (2021)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Hearing & vibration · Medium: Multisensory installation, Spatial audio
- Shown at: Liquid Ground, Para Site, Hong Kong 2021
- Idea: Hearing the wetland as a buffalo does, up to 40,000 Hz, to register the animals that turned abandoned rice fields into habitat.
- What it is: Part I of the Lantau Trilogy: field recordings made by following Lantau's feral water buffalo, captured with a binaural microphone shaped like buffalo ears and played back as a sound sculpture split into audible and ultrasonic ranges.
- How it works: Buffalo ears 3D-scanned and 3D-printed into a binaural microphone, ultrasonic field recordings, a water speaker and 4K video.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vOeUkKI-Yxw
- Images: https://static.wixstatic.com/media/d3b537_ae2a5e50def2431ea2364ef8f97c2582~mv2.jpeg https://static.wixstatic.com/media/d3b537_8e929482431047fba804a816cb9d61b6~mv2.jpg https://static.wixstatic.com/media/d3b537_f2821c91de31427fb608d7771bfbfbb7~mv2.jpg
- Project page: https://www.zhengmahler.world/bubalusbubalis16-40000hz

#### Gorilla Tag — Another Axiom (2021)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement, Collective & networked · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: Walking on your hands: remove the legs and the body learns ape locomotion through the arms, a body swap millions of players adopted.
- What it is: A multiplayer VR game in which players are legless gorillas who move only by pushing their hands against the ground, walls and trees, playing tag.
- How it works: Controller positions push a physics body with no joystick locomotion; first released in early access on Meta Quest and SteamVR, originally made by Kerestell Smith.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y3bR3s546CU
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/1533390/header.jpg
- Project page: https://gorillatagvr.com/

#### Shelter 3 — Might and Delight (2021)
- Type: Game · Senses: Collective & networked, Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Herd as body: care is extended from one calf to a whole family that moves together.
- What it is: As an elephant matriarch you lead your herd and calf across open landscapes, gathering them in danger and searching for water and shelter.
- How it works: Third-person herd simulation in which followers respond to the matriarch's calls and position.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=U_IlNFERdzE
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/977630/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.mightanddelight.com/

#### Spirit of the North — Infuse Studio (2019)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Landscape at fox height, told without words.
- What it is: A wordless adventure in which you are a red fox travelling through Icelandic landscapes, joined by a guardian spirit of the Northern Lights.
- How it works: Third-person exploration and light puzzles using a spirit ability that separates from the fox's body.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6fZBM6iKUrg
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/1213700/header.jpg
- Project page: https://playspiritofthenorth.com/

#### Becoming Animal — Emma Davie, Peter Mettler (2018)
- Type: Immersive film · Senses: Multisensory, Time & scale · Medium: Screen & web
- Shown at: CPH:DOX 2018
- Idea: Becoming animal begins with recovering our own animal senses, as Abram argues, rather than with technology.
- What it is: A documentary made with philosopher David Abram in Grand Teton National Park that slows the viewer down to listen and look at elk, ravens, insects and weather as sensing beings.
- How it works: Long takes, close sound recording and Abram's voice-over on sensory phenomenology.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Oia7EQupIZY
- Project page: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Oia7EQupIZY

#### Enfacing an Ape — Ke Ma (2018)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Screen & web
- Idea: People can take another species' face as their own, and traits flow both ways: they rated themselves less intelligent and apes more emotional.
- What it is: Participants controlled a virtual face with their own face movements while it morphed into an ape's face.
- How it works: Face tracking drives a virtual face in or out of sync, which is then morphed into an ape face (enfacement illusion).
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1007/s00426-018-1048-x (Psychological Research 2019)

#### How to Carve a Sculpture — AKI INOMATA (2018)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Touch & haptics, Body schema & movement · Medium: Multisensory installation, Spatial audio
- Idea: A human imitates an animal's making: to copy the beaver's marks, the sculptor must follow its teeth and the wood's knots.
- What it is: Wood chewed by beavers in Japanese zoos is shown beside enlarged copies carved by a human sculptor and by machine, with recordings of beavers gnawing, asking who the sculptor is.
- How it works: Beaver-gnawed wood, 3D scanning, machine and hand carving, and sound design by Yutaka Ito.
- Images: https://www.aki-inomata.com/shared/img/works/05/05-01.jpg https://www.aki-inomata.com/shared/img/works/05/05-02.jpg
- Project page: https://www.aki-inomata.com/works/how_to_make/

#### Pajé-Onça Hackeando a 33ª Bienal de Artes de São Paulo — Denilson Baniwa (2018)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Body schema & movement, Multisensory · Medium: Performance & participatory, Screen & web
- Shown at: 33rd São Paulo Biennial (uninvited intervention) 2018; Biennale of Sydney 2020 (NIRIN)
- Idea: Becoming jaguar is a shamanic and political act: the strongest shaman takes the jaguar's body to travel between worlds and to reclaim space.
- What it is: An uninvited performance in which Baniwa, dressed as the Pajé-Onça (jaguar shaman) in a jaguar-patterned robe, yellow toothed mask, flowers and rattle, walked through the 33rd São Paulo Biennial, which included no Indigenous artists.
- How it works: Live intervention with costume, mask and rattle, documented as a 15-minute HD video.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RkGNGJsshSM
- Images: https://s3.amazonaws.com/wp-sumauma.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/23160547/editcapa_pajeonca-aldeia-de-Santa-Isabel_fotografia-de-Sallisa-Rosa-1920x1275.jpg https://www.biennaleofsydney.art/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/denilsonBaniwa1-900x600.jpg
- Project page: https://www.pipaprize.com/denilson-baniwa/paje-onca-hackeando-a-33a-bienal-de-artes-de-sao-paulo-1/

#### One or Several Tigers — Ho Tzu Nyen (2017)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Hearing & vibration, Body schema & movement, Time & scale · Medium: Multisensory installation, Performance & participatory, Screen & web
- Shown at: Holland Festival 2018
- Idea: The tiger as narrator and as a body humans can turn into: colonial history told from the animal side.
- What it is: A two-channel animation and theatre work in which a Malayan tiger and a British surveyor meet in 1835 Singapore; the tiger speaks, sings and shape-shifts, drawing on the Malay figure of the weretiger (harimau jadian).
- How it works: Animated video with a shadow-puppet-like screen and a robotic, sounding tiger; staged as theatre with live projection.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sbdTL-B1qBg
- Images: https://i.ytimg.com/vi/sbdTL-B1qBg/hqdefault.jpg
- Project page: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0J9JEAMfYHE

#### Rain World — Videocult (2017)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Being low in a food chain: other creatures follow their own lives, and the player is one animal among many.
- What it is: A survival platformer in which you are a slugcat, a small prey animal in a ruined industrial ecosystem of predators, forced to find shelter before deadly rains.
- How it works: Procedurally animated creatures with autonomous AI that hunt, flee and interact independently of the player.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kxbQy0U92R4
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/312520/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.akuparagames.com/game/rain-world/

#### Being a Beast — Charles Foster (2016)
- Type: Book / theory · Senses: Smell & taste, Multisensory · Medium: Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Ig Nobel Prize in Biology 2016
- Idea: Without any device, becoming animal means re-training smell, sleep, diet and fear, and admitting what cannot be reached.
- What it is: A book recording the author's attempts to live as a badger in a Welsh hillside sett, an otter, an urban fox, a red deer hunted by hounds and a migrating swift.
- How it works: Immersive field practice: sleeping underground, eating worms, crawling on all fours, and drawing on sensory biology and neuroscience.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TIzqzrDQedY
- Project page: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Foster_(writer)

#### Invasion! — Baobab Studios (2016)
- Type: Immersive film · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset, 360° / immersive film
- Shown at: Tribeca Film Festival 2016
- Idea: The simplest embodiment cue, seeing an animal body where your own should be, is enough to shift how the scene addresses you.
- What it is: An animated VR short in which you are a small white rabbit on a frozen lake when two aliens land; looking down, you see your own furry body.
- How it works: Real-time animated VR in which the viewer's camera is placed in a rabbit avatar, with characters reacting to the viewer.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SZ0fKW5PttM
- Project page: https://www.baobabstudios.com/

#### Paws - A Shelter 2 Game — Might and Delight (2016)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: The world at cub scale: loss and friendship seen from the smallest and weakest body.
- What it is: A standalone adventure in which you are a young lynx cub finding its way home through a large world after losing its family.
- How it works: Third-person exploration with climbing and simple animal interactions in the Shelter art style.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4aQZ8G8eS9c
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/434000/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.mightanddelight.com/

#### Shelter 2 — Might and Delight (2015)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement, Collective & networked · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: A predator's parenthood: hunting is the way to keep others alive.
- What it is: As a lynx mother you give birth, hunt across tundra and forest and raise your cubs until they can survive alone.
- How it works: Open-world third-person play with hunting, cub growth across seasons and cubs that can be carried on to later play.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Tk-1M1wNVDY
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/275100/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.mightanddelight.com/

#### Goat Simulator — Coffee Stain Studios (2014)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Counterpoint: a parody of animal simulation, where the goat is an excuse for physics comedy.
- What it is: A sandbox in which you are a goat who headbutts, licks and wrecks a small town, with deliberately broken ragdoll physics.
- How it works: Ragdoll physics with a sticky tongue and headbutt, built quickly from a game-jam prototype.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JN2QUhaKN2Q
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/265930/header.jpg

#### K-9_topology: I Hunt Nature and Culture Hunts Me — Maja Smrekar (2014)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Body schema & movement, Smell & taste · Medium: Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Rencontres Bandits-Mages 2014; Prix Ars Electronica 2017 (Golden Nica, Hybrid Art, K-9_topology)
- Idea: Meeting the wolf as the dog's ancestor asks what domestication removed from both species.
- What it is: After a residency at Jacana Wildlife Studios in France, the artist performed inside an enclosure with wolves and wolfdogs, building trust through body language rather than training.
- How it works: Weeks of ethologist-guided contact with a wolf pack, then a live performance followed by a public discussion.
- Video: https://vimeo.com/111946213
- Images: https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/5c326f997c9327edb6347bf8/1547908448070-C8AMJ5OR3T2CYJARJBLH/01_I+HUNT+NATURE+AND+CULTURE+HUNTS+ME.jpg
- Project page: https://www.majasmrekar.org/k9-topology-i-hunt-nature-and-culture-hunts-me

#### Shelter — Might and Delight (2013)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement, Collective & networked · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Becoming a parent animal: care and vulnerability, not power, are the mechanics.
- What it is: As a mother badger you lead five cubs from their burrow through forest, river and fire to a new home, feeding them and guarding them from a bird of prey.
- How it works: Third-person play in a painted, low-poly world where cub AI follows the mother and can starve or be taken.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tmKm_oX8wzQ
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/244710/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.mightanddelight.com/

#### Bear 71 — Leanne Allison, Jeremy Mendes (2012)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision · Medium: Screen & web, VR headset
- Shown at: Sundance New Frontier 2012; IDFA DocLab 2012
- Idea: The wild animal's view of being watched: the bear narrates while the user moves through the camera network that tracks her.
- What it is: An interactive documentary narrated by a female grizzly in Banff National Park, known only by her tag number, whose life is seen through trail cameras and a surveillance map; later adapted for VR.
- How it works: WebGL map of Banff populated with trail-camera footage; the 2017 VR version (NFB with Jam3) turns the map into a walkable space.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2cVhaMC5Iv8
- Project page: https://www.nfb.ca/interactive/bear_71/

#### Primate Cinema: Apes as Family — Rachel Mayeri (2012)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision · Medium: Screen & web, Multisensory installation
- Shown at: Arts Catalyst London 2012
- Idea: Designing for chimpanzee attention forces the filmmaker to guess what another species finds meaningful to watch.
- What it is: A film made for the chimpanzees of Edinburgh Zoo, with human actors in ape costumes playing a family drama; the gallery version shows the film beside footage of the chimps watching it.
- How it works: Scripted with primatologists and tested on the chimpanzees' own viewing screen; two-channel video installation.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4871rINIAeQ
- Images: https://rachelmayeri.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/denise-with-remote-16x9-wide.jpg
- Project page: http://rachelmayeri.com/primate-cinema

#### WolfQuest — eduweb (2007)
- Type: Game · Senses: Smell & taste, Collective & networked · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Science-based wolf life: survival follows real ecology, and a scent view reveals prey and territory marks.
- What it is: A wildlife simulation made with the Minnesota Zoo in which you are a gray wolf in Yellowstone, hunting elk, finding a mate, defending territory and raising pups.
- How it works: 3D simulation of Yellowstone's Lamar Valley and Slough Creek with a scent-view mode and multiplayer packs, first funded by the US National Science Foundation.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dS26bwYDzmc
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/926990/header.jpg
- Project page: http://www.wolfquest.org

#### Ōkami — Clover Studio (2006)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: A wolf body with a painter's gesture: restoring nature is done by drawing, in a sumi-e world.
- What it is: An action-adventure in which you are Amaterasu, the Japanese sun goddess in the form of a white wolf, restoring a cursed land to life by painting on the world with a celestial brush.
- How it works: Cel-shaded sumi-e rendering; pausing the game turns the screen into paper on which brush strokes cast spells.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4_42UdWgEmY
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/587620/header.jpg
- Project page: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%C5%8Ckami

#### Journey to the Lower World — Marcus Coates (2004)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Body schema & movement, Hearing & vibration · Medium: Performance & participatory, Screen & web
- Shown at: Liverpool 2004
- Idea: Animal voice and costume become a practical tool to speak to a community's problem from outside the human frame.
- What it is: Wearing a deer skin, the artist performed a shamanic journey in the flat of residents of a Liverpool tower block due for demolition, calling like animals to seek advice from the spirit world, and filmed it.
- How it works: A Siberian Yakut-inspired ritual with animal skin and vocal imitation, performed live and presented as a single-channel video.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FAUWVKxiG2s
- Project page: https://www.marcuscoates.co.uk/

#### AlphaWolf — MIT Media Lab Synthetic Characters Group (2001)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Hearing & vibration, Collective & networked · Medium: Multisensory installation, Game
- Shown at: SIGGRAPH 2001 Emerging Technologies
- Idea: You join a pack with your voice: becoming wolf means learning a social order through dominance and submission sounds.
- What it is: Three visitors each play a wolf pup in a virtual pack, howling, growling, whining or barking into a microphone to shape how their pup interacts with its packmates and where it stands in the hierarchy.
- How it works: Vocal-affect classification of the visitor's sounds drives autonomous wolf agents with learning, emotion and development models.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-540-45173-0_2 (Lecture Notes in Computer Science 2003)
- Video: https://characters.media.mit.edu/video/alphaWolf.mov
- Images: https://characters.media.mit.edu/images/alphawolf.gif
- Project page: https://characters.media.mit.edu/projects/alphawolf.html

#### The Bush Soul (#1–#3) — Rebecca Allen (1997)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement, Touch & haptics · Medium: Multisensory installation, Game
- Idea: Named after the West African belief in a 'bush soul' that lives inside a wild animal, it frames entering a virtual world as sending part of yourself to live among other creatures.
- What it is: A series of three installations in which a visitor's 'soul', a pulsing sphere of energy, enters a virtual bush of artificial-life creatures that approach, avoid and respond to it; a force-feedback joystick lets the visitor feel the world.
- How it works: Custom behaviour engine (later Emergence) where each character carries 'feelings' toward objects that drive its movement, with force-feedback joystick navigation.
- Images: https://assets.locomotive.works/sites/630ddaf4586d95007d5cf2e2/content_entry630ddb1b594da90081a5673c/630ddf87586d95007d5cf3e9/files/bs3.01.jpg?1750273474 https://assets.locomotive.works/sites/630ddaf4586d95007d5cf2e2/content_entry630ddb1b594da90081a5673c/630ddf94586d95007d5cf41c/files/bs1.in.1.jpg?1661853991
- Project page: http://www.rebeccaallen.com/projects/bush-soul-number-3

#### The Virtual Reality Gorilla Exhibit — Don Allison, Larry F. Hodges (1996)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset
- Idea: You learn gorilla social rules by being treated as a gorilla, an early VR case of learning through animal embodiment.
- What it is: At Zoo Atlanta, students in a head-mounted display take the role of an adolescent gorilla inside a virtual gorilla habitat, and the virtual gorillas react to their approach with dominance and submission behaviours.
- How it works: SGI-rendered model of the Zoo Atlanta habitat with behaviour-controlled gorilla agents designed with primatologists, shown in a tracked HMD.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1109/38.626967 (IEEE Computer Graphics and Applications 1997)

#### I Like America and America Likes Me — Joseph Beuys (1974)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Performance & participatory
- Shown at: René Block Gallery New York 1974
- Idea: A counterpoint: meeting the animal on its own terms, through shared time and territory rather than imitation or simulation.
- What it is: The artist spent three days in a New York gallery room with a wild coyote, wrapped in felt with a shepherd's staff, sharing the space until the animal accepted him.
- How it works: Durational performance with felt, staff, straw and copies of the Wall Street Journal, documented on film and photographs.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IjI3_w9ZbX0
- Project page: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I_Like_America_and_America_Likes_Me

#### Unicorn (Einhorn) — Rebecca Horn (1970)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Wearable & sensory device, Performance & participatory
- Idea: A body extension that changes posture and gait until the wearer moves like another creature, an early prosthetic becoming.
- What it is: A woman walks through fields and woods wearing a tall white horn strapped to her head and torso with bandages, balancing the extension with every step.
- How it works: Wood-and-fabric horn attached with a harness of straps; performed for film.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8-uShAVpDLM

### Reptiles & amphibians

Snakes, lizards, frogs, turtles: infrared pits, chameleon eyes, cold blood.

#### Be A Chameleon — Treta Studios (2026)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: Camouflage as a verb: blending into the background is the chameleon's main action, alongside a tongue used as a limb.
- What it is: A VR game in which you are a chameleon escaping a pet shop: you swing on your sticky tongue, climb walls and change colour to hide from staff.
- How it works: Tongue grappling, hand-based climbing and colour matching against surfaces on Meta Quest, with a Steam release planned.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=x954fIuri1Y
- Images: https://queststoredb.com/media/26786967120939806_cover_landscape.webp
- Project page: https://www.meta.com/experiences/be-a-chameleon/26786967120939806/

#### Jurassic Flight (Birdly) — SOMNIACS (2020)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision, Touch & haptics · Medium: VR headset, Multisensory installation
- Idea: The same flapping body can carry an extinct flyer, so embodiment becomes a way to imagine a vanished animal's flight.
- What it is: A Birdly experience in which you embody a pterosaur, Kepodactylus, and fly over dinosaurs in a Jurassic landscape reconstructed with palaeontologists.
- How it works: Birdly prone motion platform with arm-and-hand wing control, VR headset and headwind fan; scene and animal models made in consultation with palaeontologists.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cVP5h2kyRgw
- Project page: https://www.birdlyvr.com/

#### Snake Pass — Sumo Digital (2017)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Thinking like a snake: movement is coiling and grip, and you must plan with your whole body.
- What it is: A physics puzzle-platformer in which you are Noodle the snake; with no jump, you must coil around bamboo poles and grip to climb.
- How it works: A physics-driven spine controlled with separate inputs for slithering forward, raising the head and tightening grip.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kW9uDgo7nlA
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/544330/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.sumo-digital.com/

#### Virtual Chameleon — Fumio Mizuno (2009)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Altered vision · Medium: Wearable & sensory device
- Idea: Try seeing like a chameleon, with two eyes that look different ways at once.
- What it is: A wearable system with two independently steered cameras that shows each eye a different view, controlled by the wearer.
- How it works: Two motorised cameras steered by hand-held trackers, each feeding one side of a head-mounted display.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-642-03904-1_48 (IFMBE Proceedings 2009)

#### INSN(H)AK(R)ES — Diana Domingues (2001)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Body schema & movement, Collective & networked · Medium: Multisensory installation, Screen & web
- Idea: You enter a snake enclosure as a snake, at snake height, sharing one animal body with other remote users and caring for the real snakes around it.
- What it is: A telematic installation in which remote participants share the body of a robot snake living in a serpentarium with real snakes, steering it over the web and seeing through the camera on its head.
- How it works: A snake-shaped robot (named Ângela) with an onboard web camera is teleoperated through the internet; presence sensors trigger water and food for the live snakes when the robot passes.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=v9HElj0X6vA
- Project page: https://digitalartarchive.at/database/work/5052/

### Many species

Works that let you move through the perspectives of several species.

#### What makes us most Human is also so Animal — Jiabao Li (2026)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Smell & taste, Breath & interoception · Medium: Performance & participatory, Multisensory installation
- Idea: Crossing the species line through taste: breastfeeding makes the artist most human precisely because it makes her a mammal.
- What it is: A performance and blind tasting in which visitors drink a line-up of milks and guess their source: the artist's own, cow, goat, sheep, water buffalo, camel, horse, yak, donkey, bat and coyote.
- How it works: Milk samples from eleven mammals served blind, framed by a live performance about motherhood and artificial intelligence.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8k3acKrF-o8
- Project page: https://www.jiabaoli.org/what-makes-us-most-human-is-also-so-animal

#### Face Jumping — Tender Claws (2025)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset
- Shown at: SXSW 2025 XR; Venice Immersive 2025
- Idea: Eye contact is the doorway between bodies: to become another, you first have to look it in the eye.
- What it is: A surreal VR piece in which meeting another character's gaze lets you jump into their body, moving between strangers, animals and objects on a journey of rebirth.
- How it works: Uses the eye tracking of Meta Quest Pro to detect mutual gaze and trigger perspective swaps.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SNT_MuIJ8aY
- Images: https://static.labiennale.org/files/styles/full_screen_slide/public/cinema/2025/Schede_film/970x647/Ve_Immersive/face_jumping.jpg?itok=m12qWn8f https://static.labiennale.org/files/styles/seo_thumbnail/public/cinema/2025/Schede_film/970x647/Ve_Immersive/face_jumping.jpg?itok=OnaLq0ZU
- Project page: https://www.labiennale.org/en/cinema/2025/venice-immersive/face-jumping

#### The Dream of Zhuang Zhou — Shuai Zou (2025)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Multisensory · Medium: VR headset
- Shown at: SIGGRAPH Asia 2025
- Idea: Transformative embodiment (wuhua): each species builds a different world from the same landscape.
- What it is: A VR experience built on Zhuangzi's butterfly dream in which the visitor passes between the perspectives of a human, a fish, a butterfly and a bird in a Chinese landscape.
- How it works: 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstruction of a landscape rendered with species-specific visual and cognitive mappings.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3757369.3767609 (SIGGRAPH Asia 2025 Art Papers)

#### AnimalSense — Yu-Lun Hsu (2024)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Echolocation, Electroreception, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: Learn an animal sense by having to use it.
- What it is: A VR game with levels in which players use a bat's echolocation, an eel's electroreception and a jumping spider's panoramic vision to solve challenges.
- How it works: Sensory substitution and remapping in VR: echoes rendered as spatial sound and visuals, electric fields as haptics, panoramic vision compressed into the headset view.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3613905.3648102 (CHI 2024 Extended Abstracts)
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OycDmP3hobQ

#### Becoming an Animal? Proteus Effect and Hand Gestures — Tangjun Qu (2024)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset
- Idea: The body you wear can reshape the body you have, down to hand posture.
- What it is: Participants wore human or animal hands in VR; a gesture-recognition model measured whether their real hands started to move like the animal's.
- How it works: Hand tracking in a headset with three animal hand models; a trained classifier scores gesture consistency with each avatar.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1109/ismar62088.2024.00093 (IEEE ISMAR 2024)

#### Morphogenic Angels: Chapter 1 — Keiken (2023)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Touch & haptics, Hearing & vibration, Body schema & movement · Medium: Multisensory installation, Game, Wearable & sensory device
- Shown at: HAU Hebbel am Ufer, Berlin 2023; PHI Montreal 2025; BredaPhoto 2026; Baltic Centre for Contemporary Art 2026
- Idea: The belly becomes a listening organ: haptics are used as 'tools of consciousness' to feel beings you cannot see or understand.
- What it is: A video-game installation set 1,000 years ahead, when humans have become 'Angels' able to embody other kinds of consciousness; visitors wear a haptic 'womb' on the belly that vibrates with sounds shifting between underwater mammals, sandstorms and space.
- How it works: Game-engine world and CGI film in a black-sand set, with Keiken's wearable haptic womb synced to headphone audio.
- Video: https://vimeo.com/851219516
- Images: https://keiken.cloud/wp-content/uploads/2023/08/keiken-Morphogenic-Angels-2023-Hau-Berlin-Lowres-10.jpg
- Project page: https://keiken.cloud/work/morphogenic-angels-chapter-1/

#### Tchia — Awaceb (2023)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Soul jumping: birds, dogs, fish, rocks and lamps can all be inhabited, each with its own abilities.
- What it is: An open-world game set in an archipelago inspired by New Caledonia, where a 'Soul Jump' lets you take control of any animal or object.
- How it works: A timed possession mechanic swaps the player's controls to the target creature's or object's movement set.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=rdAGrXZC2iQ
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/1496590/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.awaceb.com/tchia

#### Plastisapiens — Miri Chekhanovich, Édith Jorisch (2022)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement, Touch & haptics · Medium: VR headset
- Shown at: Tribeca Immersive 2022; IDFA DocLab 2022
- Idea: Becoming a plastic-permeated body: embodiment used for speculative, feminist eco-fiction rather than empathy.
- What it is: A surreal interactive VR eco-fiction in which the participant embodies animals and altered humans whose bodies have absorbed plastic and become more fluid.
- How it works: Real-time VR with body-tracked avatars that deform and merge; produced by the NFB and DPT.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UKuUlpfUi68
- Project page: https://voicesofvr.com/1105-tribeca-xr-embodiment-experiments-in-a-surrealist-speculative-future-feminist-eco-fiction-on-plastics-permeating-the-body-in-plastisapians/

#### Myriad. Where We Connect — Lena Thiele (2021)
- Type: Immersive film · Senses: Time & scale, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset, 360° / immersive film
- Shown at: Venice VR Expanded 2021; Wildscreen Festival 2022
- Idea: Travelling as a migrating animal makes the planet legible as currents, routes and distances rather than places.
- What it is: A VR journey along the real migration routes of a Northern Bald Ibis, an Arctic fox and a green sea turtle, following wind and ocean currents across a world made of carbon.
- How it works: Real-time VR and 360° versions driven by scientific GPS tracking data of the three species.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bhAM7tUr-Dg
- Images: https://static.labiennale.org/files/styles/seo_thumbnail/public/cinema/2021/Schede_film/970x647/Venice_VR_Expanded/thiele_myriad-ok2.jpg?itok=Jw687wrD
- Project page: https://www.labiennale.org/en/cinema/2021/lineup/venice-vr-expanded/myriad-where-we-connect-vr-experience

#### Refuge for Resurgence — Superflux (2021)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Multisensory installation
- Shown at: Venice Architecture Biennale 2021; Our Time on Earth, Barbican 2022
- Idea: Taking a seat as one species among many: design that asks visitors to imagine the table from other animals' needs.
- What it is: A multispecies banquet table with seats, crockery and cutlery designed for fourteen species — from humans to wolf, rat, pigeon, moss and fungi — set in a post-flood home.
- How it works: Hand-carved wooden table and custom ceramic, glass and metal tableware for each species.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=s637PPO3Wl4
- Project page: https://superflux.in

#### Samsara — Hsin-Chien Huang (2021)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset
- Shown at: Venice VR Expanded 2021
- Idea: Reincarnation as embodied cognition: each rebirth gives you a new body and a new perspective on the same planet.
- What it is: After humans destroy the Earth and re-engineer their DNA to survive in space, the participant returns to Earth in another era as a different life form, then passes through other beings' bodies in a cycle of rebirth.
- How it works: Free-roaming VR with full-body avatars that change species across chapters (HTC Vive / Quest).
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9IygU6BpINQ
- Images: https://static.labiennale.org/files/styles/seo_thumbnail/public/cinema/2021/Schede_film/970x647/Venice_VR_Expanded/huang_samsara.jpg?itok=EaPJB35o
- Project page: https://www.labiennale.org/en/cinema/2021/lineup/venice-vr-expanded/lun-hui-samsara

#### Beyond Human: Animals as an Escape from Stereotype Avatars — Andrey Krekhov (2019)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement, Altered vision · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: Game mechanics can be built from what an animal body can do, not from human templates.
- What it is: Players became a scorpion, a rhino and a bird, each with its own body parts and abilities, such as a stinging tail or flight.
- How it works: Room-scale VR with body-tracked mappings of extra body parts (tail, wings) and animal-specific senses and movement.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3311350.3347172 (CHI PLAY 2019)
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fa2Ivv14GL4
- Images: https://ar5iv.labs.arxiv.org/html/1907.07466/assets/figures/teaser.jpg https://ar5iv.labs.arxiv.org/html/1907.07466/assets/figures/scorpion.jpg

#### Lost Ember — Mooneye Studios (2019)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Switching bodies: each animal opens a different way of moving through the same world.
- What it is: An adventure in which you are a wolf who can inhabit other animals, such as a wombat, duck, fish and hummingbird, to cross a ruined land with a spirit companion.
- How it works: Third-person play where the player jumps into nearby animals, each with its own movement set.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vo7CPfVu7MY
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/563840/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.lostember.com

#### The Illusion of Animal Body Ownership — Andrey Krekhov (2019)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: Non-humanoid bodies can produce ownership as strong as, and sometimes stronger than, a human avatar.
- What it is: Players controlled a virtual spider, bat and other creatures with different body-tracking mappings and reported how much the body felt like their own.
- How it works: Full-body tracking with mappings of human arms and legs onto eight spider legs or bat wings, viewed in a virtual mirror.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1109/cig.2019.8848005 (IEEE Conference on Games (CoG) 2019)
- Images: https://ar5iv.labs.arxiv.org/html/1907.05220/assets/figures/modes.jpg https://ar5iv.labs.arxiv.org/html/1907.05220/assets/figures/scene.jpg

#### Inside Tumucumaque — Interactive Media Foundation (2018)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Echolocation, Time & scale · Medium: VR headset, Multisensory installation
- Shown at: ZKM Karlsruhe 2018; Raindance Film Festival 2018 (Special Jury Mention); ADC Gold 2019; European Design Award Gold 2019; VR Days Europe 2019
- Idea: Each animal is a different translation of the same place: ultraviolet colour, slow motion, echolocation or night vision.
- What it is: A VR installation set in a clearing of the Brazilian rainforest where you switch between five animals, a black caiman, harpy eagle, vampire bat, poison dart frog and Goliath birdeater, and perceive the forest through each one's senses.
- How it works: Models and animations were built with scientists of the Museum für Naturkunde Berlin; senses are rendered as ultraviolet palettes, super-slow motion, sonar visualisation, colour night vision and 3D sound.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=rFkcRQq_UT4
- Images: https://inside-tumucumaque.com/images/multi_e.jpg
- Project page: https://inside-tumucumaque.com/

#### Mulaka — Lienzo (2018)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: Game
- Shown at: PSX 2017; PAX West 2017
- Idea: Shape-shifting as Indigenous knowledge: the shaman takes on animal bodies to fly, break stone and climb, each form a different way of moving through the land.
- What it is: An action-adventure game in which the player is a Sukurúame, a Rarámuri shaman, who crosses landscapes modelled on the Sierra Tarahumara and draws on the powers of demigods that take animal forms.
- How it works: 3D low-poly platformer for consoles and PC in which transformation powers (likely including a bird form for flight and a heavy animal form for breaking obstacles) are unlocked from demigods.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eGYUecSvPoY
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/623640/ss_f28c3b3045cd9a622992f0808d773ebda49b7d86.1920x1080.jpg?t=1584052040
- Project page: https://store.steampowered.com/app/623640/Mulaka/

#### VR Animals: Surreal Body Ownership in VR Games — Andrey Krekhov (2018)
- Type: Paper · Senses: Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset, Game
- Idea: Body ownership is not limited to human-shaped avatars: players can feel an animal body as theirs.
- What it is: A first exploratory study of playing as three animals in VR with five control schemes, from a third-person companion view to first-person full-body tracking.
- How it works: HTC Vive with full-body trackers mapping human limbs to animal limbs; compared with controller and companion modes.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3270316.3271531 (CHI PLAY 2018 Extended Abstracts)

#### Everything — David OReilly (2017)
- Type: Game · Senses: Time & scale, Collective & networked · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Shown at: Vienna Shorts 2017 (jury award, first game eligible for an Academy Award)
- Idea: Becoming as a continuum: every thing can be inhabited, and the main move is changing scale rather than acting.
- What it is: A game in which you can become anything in the universe, from animals and plants to planets, galaxies and atoms, moving freely between scales while recordings of Alan Watts play.
- How it works: A procedural world of thousands of low-poly things that the player can 'ascend' to larger or 'descend' to smaller; animals roll rather than walk.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aIMlcRCjjPw
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/582270/header.jpg
- Project page: http://www.everything-game.com/

#### Life of Us — Within, Chris Milk (2017)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Body schema & movement, Hearing & vibration · Medium: VR headset
- Shown at: Sundance New Frontier 2017
- Idea: Evolution as a sequence of bodies you wear with a friend; seeing the other person's creature tells you what you have become.
- What it is: A shared VR journey through evolution in which two participants embody a chain of creatures — single cell, fish, frog, pterodactyl, ape, human and beyond — with their voices modulated to match each body.
- How it works: Real-time multi-user VR with tracked hands and real-time voice modulation for each creature.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RpyFs6O-328
- Project page: https://www.with.in

#### Meadow - A Shelter Game — Might and Delight (2016)
- Type: Game · Senses: Collective & networked, Hearing & vibration · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Social life without language: strangers become a herd through sound and body alone.
- What it is: A multiplayer game in which players, as badgers, rabbits, deer and other animals, meet anonymously and communicate only through animal calls and gestures.
- How it works: Online multiplayer with no text or voice chat; expression is limited to animal sounds, emotes and following one another.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pYC0nQNRgvE
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/486310/header.jpg
- Project page: https://www.mightanddelight.com/

#### The Great Animal Orchestra — Bernie Krause, United Visual Artists (2016)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Hearing & vibration, Altered vision · Medium: Multisensory installation, Spatial audio
- Shown at: Fondation Cartier pour l'art contemporain Paris 2016; Exploratorium San Francisco
- Idea: Listening to a habitat as an orchestra reveals the soundscape as a shared, divided space among species.
- What it is: A dark room where recordings of wild habitats, from Borneo to Zimbabwe to California, play in surround sound while their spectrograms scroll across the walls, showing how each species occupies its own band of the soundscape.
- How it works: Five decades of Krause's field recordings rendered by UVA as real-time spectrogram projections with multichannel sound.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=o1SnSv0OQdY
- Project page: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bernie_Krause

#### In the Eyes of the Animal — Marshmallow Laser Feast, Abandon Normal Devices (2015)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Hearing & vibration, Touch & haptics · Medium: VR headset, Multisensory installation, 360° / immersive film
- Shown at: AND Festival, Grizedale Forest 2015; Sundance New Frontier 2016; Sónar 2016; Supersenses, National Science and Media Museum, Bradford 2017; Singapore International Festival of Arts 2017; Particles of Existence, Phi Centre, Montreal 2018; Timber Festival 2018; Coda Festival 2019; OMM, Eskişehir 2019; Discovery Naturescape, Hwaseong 2022; BBK Klima Abentura, Bilbao 2023; Schemerlicht Festival, Nijmegen 2024; Sentients, Watershed, Bristol 2026
- Idea: Each animal is a different forest: the mosquito sees plumes of carbon dioxide, the dragonfly sees at 240 frames per second, the owl sees only what it faces.
- What it is: A VR journey through Grizedale Forest along the food chain, seen successively through the senses of a mosquito, a dragonfly, a frog and an owl. Visitors wear a headset inside a sculptural helmet and a vibrating backpack while a binaural forest soundscape plays.
- How it works: LiDAR scans, CT scans, photogrammetry and 360° drone footage of Grizedale Forest rendered in real time in vvvv, with a MaxMSP binaural engine, a SubPac haptic backpack for wingbeats and croaks, and scent; later released as a 360° film.
- Video: https://vimeo.com/140057053
- Images: https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/app/uploads/2024/07/Screenshot-2024-07-24-at-10.52.46.jpg https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/app/uploads/2024/07/ITEOTA_180Renders_0147_4k.jpg https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/app/uploads/2024/07/Screenshot-2024-07-24-at-11.02.24.jpg
- Project page: https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/project/in-the-eyes-of-the-animal/

#### FlyVIZ: 360° Vision — Anatole Lécuyer (2012)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Altered vision · Medium: Wearable & sensory device, VR headset
- Shown at: SIGGRAPH 2016 Emerging Technologies
- Idea: Humans can adapt to eyes in the back of their heads.
- What it is: A helmet that gives the wearer real-time 360° vision of their surroundings, like the panoramic sight of a fly or a prey animal.
- How it works: Panoramic camera on top of the head, with omnidirectional images remapped into a head-mounted display's field of view.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/2407336.2407344 (ACM VRST 2012)
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5Dm0wH9TDjw

#### Tokyo Jungle — Crispy's! (2012)
- Type: Game · Senses: Time & scale, Body schema & movement · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Generational survival: each animal lives a short life and the player continues as its offspring.
- What it is: A survival game set in a Tokyo without people, where you play as Pomeranians, deer, lions and dozens of other animals, eating, marking territory and breeding the next generation.
- How it works: Side-on survival with hunger, territory marking, mating and aging over in-game years; co-developed with Sony's Japan Studio for PlayStation 3.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=zasefwWK5fA
- Images: https://i.ytimg.com/vi/zasefwWK5fA/hqdefault.jpg
- Project page: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tokyo_Jungle

#### Designs for an Overpopulated Planet: Foragers — Dunne & Raby (2009)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Smell & taste, Body schema & movement · Medium: Wearable & sensory device
- Shown at: HUMAN+, Science Gallery Dublin 2011
- Idea: Becoming animal as survival: the human body is redesigned with organs of cows, birds and rodents.
- What it is: Speculative devices, including artificial rumens and bird-like filters, worn by urban foragers who have borrowed digestive systems from other animals to eat what humans cannot.
- How it works: Prototype props and photographs presenting wearable fermentation and filtering devices.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_-kLwHS__1s
- Project page: http://dunneandraby.co.uk/content/projects/510/0

#### Animal Superpowers — Chris Woebken, Kenichi Okada (2008)
- Type: Research prototype · Senses: Altered vision, Magnetic sense, Body schema & movement · Medium: Wearable & sensory device
- Shown at: Design and the Elastic Mind, MoMA New York 2008
- Idea: Each device borrows one animal sense and fits it to a child's body and play, so curiosity becomes the interface.
- What it is: A set of wearable devices for children: an ant apparatus that magnifies vision 50 times through hand-mounted microscopes, a bird device that vibrates toward a chosen direction, and a giraffe device that lowers the voice and raises the eyes by 30 cm; bat goggles for hearing ultrasound followed in 2015.
- How it works: Hand-mounted microscope cameras feeding a head display, a GPS unit driving vibration, voice pitch shifting with raised stilts, and an ultrasonic bat detector.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L9oTcez2CXU
- Images: https://freight.cargo.site/t/original/i/ad1784c02faa9d2afb6614fa878b4982436d2d58292e1349ebee13b3d8c6a682/2232862656_cba3f094c1_o.jpg https://freight.cargo.site/t/original/i/067d74e44844e7aca2661cd3baab576aa3fb852d9130e7525a1d6fce5ad67c0d/animals3.jpg
- Project page: https://www.chriswoebken.com/animal-superpowers

#### Spore — Maxis (2008)
- Type: Game · Senses: Body schema & movement, Time & scale · Medium: Game, Screen & web
- Idea: Design a body, then inhabit it: procedural animation makes any limb layout walk, eat and dance.
- What it is: A game in which you evolve a species from a single cell to a space-faring civilisation, designing its body in a creature editor and then living in it.
- How it works: A creature editor with procedural animation that adapts gait and gestures to whatever body the player builds; led by Will Wright.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=zi2GvqboQfY
- Images: https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/17390/header.jpg
- Project page: http://www.spore.com/

#### Animal-Cams — Sam Easterson (1998)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Body schema & movement · Medium: Screen & web, Multisensory installation
- Shown at: Walker Art Center Minneapolis 1998; Grand Arts Kansas City 2003; Nature Holds My Camera, Indianapolis Museum of Art; Videonale 14 Bonn 2013
- Idea: The camera rides the animal's gait and height, so the viewer feels its rhythm of movement even without its senses.
- What it is: An ongoing library of short videos shot by small cameras attached to animals, from a sheep, bison, wolf and turkey to a tarantula and scorpion, plus Burrow-Cams placed inside dens and burrows.
- How it works: Modified miniature security cameras fixed temporarily with adhesive, rubber bands or harnesses, with the help of animal handlers and wildlife experts.
- Images: https://www.grandarts.com/past_projects/2003/images/2003_01_sm01.jpg https://www.lightwork.org/uploads/2016/01/UVP_SamEasterson_BURROWCAMS_BetweenSpecies_WP-572x321.jpg
- Project page: https://www.grandarts.com/past_projects/2003/2003_01.html

#### Menagerie — Susan Amkraut, Michael Girard, Scott Fisher (1993)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Body schema & movement · Medium: VR headset
- Shown at: Revue Virtuelle, Centre Pompidou 1993
- Idea: A landmark of sharing space with autonomous animals rather than steering them: the visitor becomes one more body the flock reacts to.
- What it is: A head-mounted virtual environment populated by computer-generated birds, dogs and other creatures that flock, walk and scatter around the visitor as they move.
- How it works: Behavioural flocking and procedural legged-locomotion animation rendered in real time to a tracked stereoscopic display (likely a BOOM or HMD).
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZKETFeraZFk

#### Placeholder — Brenda Laurel, Rachel Strickland (1993)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Body schema & movement, Hearing & vibration · Medium: VR headset
- Shown at: Banff Centre for the Arts 1993
- Idea: Each creature is a different perceptual and locomotor rule set, which makes embodiment a matter of senses and movement, not appearance.
- What it is: A two-person VR work in filmed landscapes of Banff where participants could take on the body of a spirit animal, Spider, Snake, Fish or Crow, gaining its way of seeing, moving and speaking, and leave spoken 'voicemarks' for others.
- How it works: Head-mounted displays, 3D spatial audio, hand-held controllers and voice filters; Crow flies with flapping arms and Snake sees in infrared-like colour.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/192593.192637 (ACM Multimedia 1994)
- Images: https://image.jimcdn.com/app/cms/image/transf/none/path/s1cb8d6527de0e9b6/image/ifd4c0c7fa4d79286/version/1571836952/image.jpg
- Project page: https://www.tauzero.com/Brenda_Laurel/Severed_Heads/CGQ_Placeholder.html

#### Crittercam — Greg Marshall (1987)
- Type: Product · Senses: Altered vision, Body schema & movement · Medium: Screen & web
- Idea: The animal carries the camera: point-of-view footage that many immersive animal works later imitate.
- What it is: A camera and data logger attached to sharks, turtles, seals, whales, penguins and lions that records video from the animal's own body as it hunts, dives and socialises.
- How it works: Pressure-rated housing with video, depth, temperature and speed sensors, attached by suction cup, harness or clamp and recovered after release.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q-EONusvq_8

## Becoming Fungi

Networked and microbial life: mycelium, slime mould, bacteria, cells and symbiosis, at slow time and tiny scale.

### Mycelium & mushrooms

Fungal networks, spores, decomposition and the wood-wide web.

#### Mushroom Clouds — Zheng Mahler (2026)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Multisensory, Time & scale · Medium: Multisensory installation
- Shown at: PHD Group, Hong Kong 2026
- Idea: Moves from animal to fungal perception: the visitor enters humidity, fog and slow growth on the mushrooms' terms.
- What it is: Part III of the Lantau Trilogy: a 3.65 m living vivarium of Lantau plants and fruiting mushrooms, wrapped in sensor-driven fog, that asks what it is like to live, sense and feel as a mushroom.
- How it works: Vivarium with living Ganoderma, oyster, split-gill, wood-ear and lion's mane mushrooms, humidity and temperature sensors, a humidifier and a volumetric fog display.
- Images: https://static.wixstatic.com/media/d3b537_699f82e9317a4be7af6733a31bbb199b~mv2.jpg https://static.wixstatic.com/media/d3b537_162d42060f7e4c008d68f2290a82149f~mv2.jpg https://static.wixstatic.com/media/d3b537_445c1a6410b44c3abc0df9c19f5b868d~mv2.jpg
- Project page: https://www.zhengmahler.world/mushroomclouds

#### FungiSync — Botao 'Amber' Hu, Danlin Huang, Reality Design Lab (2024)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Collective & networked, Altered vision, Touch & haptics · Medium: Mixed reality, Performance & participatory
- Shown at: DevCon 2024 “Trusting the Unseen”, Bangkok; SIGGRAPH 2025 Immersive Pavilion
- Idea: Becoming fungi means becoming a node: perception is traded and mixed through contact rather than owned by one body.
- What it is: A participatory mixed-reality ritual: participants wear mushroom-decorated MR masks, each showing a different visual worldview, and blend those worldviews with others through ritual handshakes, like nodes in a mycorrhizal network.
- How it works: Resin-printed mushroom masks mounted on HoloKit headsets; the HoloField co-located multiplayer framework over Multipeer Connectivity syncs Unity VFX Graph visuals, driven live by a DJ and composited for spectators.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3721245.3734040 (SIGGRAPH 2025 Immersive Pavilion)
- Video: https://vimeo.com/1027901359
- Images: https://reality.design/media/20241120%20fungi%205225.jpg https://reality.design/media/project-fungisync-figure-01.jpg https://arxiv.org/html/2511.12533v3/images/fungisync.jpg
- Project page: https://reality.design/project/fungisync

#### Poetics of Soil: Fly Agaric I — Marshmallow Laser Feast (2024)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Time & scale, Hearing & vibration · Medium: Multisensory installation, Screen & web
- Shown at: Parallel Worlds, Gazelli Art House, Baku (COP29) 2024; SOIL: The World at Our Feet, Somerset House, London 2025; Mozilla Festival, Barcelona 2025; Only Trees Know, Natural History Museum, Shanghai 2026; More than Human, ArtScience Museum, Singapore 2026–27
- Idea: Soil is shown as a living network of fungi, bacteria and roots, inviting the viewer to consider what it is to be 'something other than human'.
- What it is: The first work in an ongoing video series on life underground, following the fly agaric mushroom and its symbiosis with trees, narrated in the words and voice of mycologist Merlin Sheldrake; Fly Agaric II and Dissolving Forest (2024) belong to the same series.
- How it works: Multichannel video and audio built from macro footage, scans and simulation of fungal growth and decomposition (details of capture not published).
- Video: https://vimeo.com/1024704240
- Images: https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/app/uploads/2024/12/Poetics-of-Soil_-Fly-Agaric-I-Detail_Marshmallow-Laser-Feast.jpg https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/app/uploads/2025/01/POS_sh05_comp_v019_lit.3281.jpg https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/app/uploads/2025/01/SCiampone-08964.jpg https://static-assets.artlogic.net/w_1600,h_1600,c_limit,f_auto,fl_lossy,q_auto/artlogicstorage/gazelli/images/view/877651cca3ff321ab75462d210a9d14ep/gazelliarthouse-marshmallow-laser-feast-dissolving-forest-2024.png
- Project page: https://marshmallowlaserfeast.com/project/poetics-of-soil-fly-agaric-i-video-installation/

#### Symbiosis/\Dysbiosis: Sentience — Tosca Terán (2024)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Collective & networked, Hearing & vibration, Multisensory · Medium: VR headset, Multisensory installation, Performance & participatory
- Shown at: Venice Immersive 2024
- Idea: Human brain signals and fungal signals are mixed in one loop, so the visitor becomes one node of a mycelial network.
- What it is: An expanded-reality open-world mushroom adventure in which real-time fungal biodata and the EEG of guests together shape an evolving soundscape and forest.
- How it works: Living mycelium wired to a custom synthesizer, EEG headsets on guests, VR world plus projection-mapped floor and walls; co-directed with Brendan Lehman.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0PzZXrqncsY
- Images: https://voicesofvr.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/symbiosisdysbiosis-sentience-970x546.jpg https://static.labiennale.org/files/styles/seo_thumbnail/public/cinema/2024/Schede_film/970x647/Ve_Immersive/symbiosis-dysbiosis.jpg?itok=FPo8HeFR
- Project page: https://voicesofvr.com/1432-combining-biodata-with-open-world-mushroom-adventure-with-symbiosis-dysbiosis-sentience/

#### Entangled Landscape — Studio Above&Below (2023)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Altered vision, Collective & networked · Medium: Mixed reality, Multisensory installation
- Idea: Makes the cooperative underground network of fungi and roots perceptible at human scale, so collaboration becomes something you watch grow or fail.
- What it is: A meditative mixed-reality piece that renders the microscopic exchange of resources in soil as a digital sculpture, driven by two neural networks trained on mycorrhizal mutualism and live environmental data from Flanders.
- How it works: Two neural networks trained on mutualistic interactions respond to live soil and weather data and render in mixed reality; developed with soil scientists.
- Video: https://vimeo.com/800511752
- Images: https://i.vimeocdn.com/video/1614942915-e8fe40e0506ee8865990ede23f0b4be8cc884726be5aa110b90c4ba9d94f1e78-d_1280x720
- Project page: https://www.studioaboveandbelow.com/work/entangled-landscape

#### Forager — Winslow Porter, Elie Zananiri (2023)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Time & scale, Smell & taste, Touch & haptics · Medium: VR headset, Multisensory installation
- Shown at: SXSW 2023; SIGGRAPH 2023 Immersive Pavilion
- Idea: Fungal time is compressed into minutes and made spatial, so growth and decomposition are lived from inside.
- What it is: A multisensory VR story in which you begin as a spore, spread as mycelium through a rotting stump and burst out as a mushroom, over the four stages of a fungus's life.
- How it works: Volumetric time-lapse of real mushrooms (automated photogrammetry rendered as voxels) in a game engine, with scent, wind haptics and SubPac bass haptics.
- Paper: https://doi.org/10.1145/3588027.3603531 (SIGGRAPH 2023 Immersive Pavilion)
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Vw_c_7Hw-DQ
- Images: https://voicesofvr.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/forager-950x534.jpg https://static.labiennale.org/files/styles/seo_thumbnail/public/cinema/2023/Schede_film/970x647/Ve_Immersive/porter-gra.jpg?itok=5DLfU6OX
- Project page: https://voicesofvr.com/1189-forager-volumetric-timelapse-of-mushroom-growth-hits-a-sweet-spot-of-touch-smell-immersive-storytelling/

#### Forest UnderSound — Tosca Terán (2021)
- Type: Artwork · Senses: Hearing & vibration, Time & scale · Medium: Multisensory installation, Spatial audio
- Shown at: Prix Ars Electronica 2021 (Honorary Mention, Digital Musics & Sound Art); The Museum Kitchener 2021; Fermynwoods Contemporary Art 2023
- Idea: The underground network becomes an audible musician on its own seasonal timescale.
- What it is: A year-long installation whose generative soundscape is played by living fungi and plant roots, changing with the seasons as they grow.
- How it works: Electrodes on mycelium and roots read bioelectric fluctuations that control analog and digital synthesizers in real time; recordings made at each equinox.
- Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1NVurUmtZk
- Project page: https://www.toscateran.com/forest-undersound

#### Mycelia — Tosca Terán (2021)
- Type: Performance · Senses: Hearing & vibration, Collective & networked · Medium: VR headset, Performance & participatory, Spatial audio
- Shown at: AMAZE Festival 2021; Raindance Immersive 2021
- Idea: The fungus becomes a performer whose signals shape a shared virtual space.
- What it is: A live VRChat performance in which the electrical activity of living mycelium drives music and an audio-reactive world, interpreted by a full-body-tracked dancer.
