- **Bartholomäus Traubeck** (1) — Media artist. German-born media artist who translates natural structures into sound and data. https://traubeck.com
- **BeAnotherLab** (1) — Art and research collective. Interdisciplinary collective that makes embodiment experiences with VR, performance and neuroscience. https://beanotherlab.org
- **Beatriz da Costa** (1) — Artist and researcher, UC Irvine (1974–2012). Tactical-media artist working across biology, activism and engineering; co-founder of Critical Art Ensemble-adjacent projects and author of Tactical Biopolitics.
- **Behnaz Farahi** (1) — Designer and creative technologist. Iranian-born designer, architect and creative technologist who builds shape-changing, sensing garments with soft robotics and computer vision. https://behnazfarahi.com
- **Ben Esposito** (1) — Independent game designer, Los Angeles. Game designer who made Donut County; earlier worked on The Unfinished Swan. http://donutcounty.com
- **Ben Joseph Andrews** (1) — Director and XR artist. Australian director who with producer Emma Roberts made Gondwana, a 24-hour durational VR simulation of the Daintree rainforest. https://gondwanavr.com
- **Bernhard Hommel** (1) — Cognitive psychologist. Cognitive psychologist known for the theory of event coding and work on action and self-representation.
- **Bernie Krause** (1) — Soundscape ecologist and musician. American musician and soundscape ecologist who coined the terms biophony and geophony and has recorded wild habitats since 1968.
- **Bianca Kennedy** (1) — Artist. German artist who works with The Swan Collective on hand-sculpted, photogrammetry-captured VR.
- **Bill Fontana** (1) — Sound sculptor. American artist who relays live sound and vibration from rivers, bridges and machines into buildings. https://resoundings.org
- **Bill Vorn** (1) — Artist, robotic art. Artist and professor at Concordia University who makes robotic installations and performances.
- **BlueTwelve Studio** (1) — Independent game studio, southern France. Studio founded by Koola and Viv, whose first game Stray casts the player as a cat in a city of robots. https://stray.game
- **Breaking Walls** (1) — Independent game studio. Studio that made AWAY: The Survival Series, a game styled as a nature documentary. https://www.breakingwalls.co/
- **Breakpoint One** (1) — XR studio. German XR studio that made VR Plant Journey with the Leibniz Institute of Plant Genetics and Crop Plant Research (IPK). https://breakpoint.one
- **Brenda Laurel** (1) — Designer, researcher and author of Computers as Theatre. American interaction designer and researcher, author of Computers as Theatre and co-founder of Purple Moon; produced the VR work Placeholder at Interval Research.
- **Brett Graham** (1) — Māori sculptor. Ngāti Korokī Kahukura sculptor; represented Aotearoa New Zealand at the Venice Biennale 2024 (Wastewater... Tai Moana Tai Tangata).
- **Brigitte Baptiste** (1) — Biologist and queer ecologist. Colombian biologist, former director of the Humboldt Institute and rector of Universidad EAN, known for queer ecology.
- **Britt Hatzius** (1) — Artist working with performance and film. German artist based in Brussels whose performances and films explore perception, language and participation.
- **Bruno Munari** (1) — Artist and designer (1907–1998). Italian artist, designer and educator, known for useless machines, children's books and playful design research.
- **Cat Jones** (1) — Artist working with body illusion and scent. Australian artist, performer and writer working with body illusion, olfaction and neuroscience; Sadakichi Award for experimental work with scent (2016). https://catjones.net
- **Charles Foster** (1) — Writer, veterinarian and barrister. British writer, veterinary surgeon and Fellow of Green Templeton College, Oxford; author of Being a Beast, for which he shared the 2016 Ig Nobel Prize in Biology.
- **Chris Campkin** (1) — Creative collaborator (Vision3). Key collaborator on Critical Distance at Vision3.
- **Chris Watson** (1) — Wildlife sound recordist. British recordist, founding member of Cabaret Voltaire and long-time BBC natural history sound recordist.
- **Chris Woebken** (1) — Designer and futures researcher. German designer in New York, co-founder of the Extrapolation Factory; his work is in the MoMA collection. https://www.chriswoebken.com/
- **Christina Kubisch** (1) — Sound artist. German composer and sound artist who has worked with electromagnetic induction since the 1970s. https://christinakubisch.de
- **Clover Studio** (1) — Capcom game studio, Osaka (2004-2007). Short-lived Capcom studio led by Atsushi Inaba and Hideki Kamiya; made Viewtiful Joe and Ōkami.
- **Coffee Stain Studios** (1) — Game studio, Skövde. Swedish studio behind Goat Simulator and Satisfactory. https://www.coffeestainstudios.com/
- **Crispy's!** (1) — Game studio, Tokyo. Studio founded by Yohei Kataoka; co-developed Tokyo Jungle with Sony's Japan Studio.
- **Croteam** (1) — Game studio. Croatian studio behind Serious Sam and The Talos Principle.
- **Cyborg Nest** (1) — Sensory augmentation company. Company founded by Liviu Babitz and Scott Cohen, with Neil Harbisson and Moon Ribas as early collaborators, that made the North Sense.
- **Cyril Laurier** (1) — Composer and data artist. French-Spanish composer and data artist working with generative sound and AI.
- **Céleste Boursier-Mougenot** (1) — Artist and composer of living sound installations. French artist and composer who represented France at the 2015 Venice Biennale and makes sound works played by birds, water and wind.
- **DRIFT** (1) — Art studio of Lonneke Gordijn and Ralph Nauta. Dutch studio founded in 2007 that builds kinetic sculptures and performances translating natural behaviour, such as starling murmurations and blooming flowers, into machines. https://studiodrift.com
- **Daan Roosegaarde** (1) — Artist and designer. Dutch designer whose Studio Roosegaarde makes large light works about landscape and climate. https://www.studioroosegaarde.net
- **Daito Manabe** (1) — Artist and programmer, Rhizomatiks. Japanese artist and programmer, co-founder of Rhizomatiks, working with data, bodies and electrical stimulation. https://daito.ws
- **Danfung Dennis** (1) — Filmmaker. American filmmaker and founder of Condition One; directed the This Is Climate Change VR series.
- **Dani Clode** (1) — Designer, Plasticity Lab (University of Cambridge / UCL). Dani Clode designed the Third Thumb as a graduate project at the Royal College of Art and developed it with Tamar Makin's lab. https://www.daniclode.com
- **Danyang Peng** (1) — HCI researcher, Keio University Graduate School of Media Design. Danyang Peng designs haptic navigation inspired by animal senses.
- **Data Garden** (1) — Plant-music company. Company founded by Joe Patitucci that makes MIDI Sprout and PlantWave, devices that turn plant bioelectric signals into music. https://plantwave.com
- **David Chaseling** (1) — Independent VR developer. Independent developer who co-created the VR octopus platformer I Am Octopus with Dylan Van Beek.
- **David Eagleman** (1) — Neuroscientist, Stanford; co-founder of Neosensory. Neuroscientist and author who argues the brain can learn new senses through the skin, and built the VEST with Scott Novich. https://eagleman.com
- **David Rothenberg** (1) — Musician and philosopher of interspecies music. American clarinettist, philosopher and professor at the New Jersey Institute of Technology, author of Why Birds Sing, who plays live with birds, whales and insects.
- **Dean A. Waters** (1) — Bat biologist, University of Leeds / York. Dean Waters studies bat echolocation and built early bat-sonar models for human navigation in virtual worlds.
- **Denilson Baniwa** (1) — Baniwa artist, curator and activist. Born in the village of Darí on the Rio Negro, Amazonas; works across painting, murals, performance and digital media to assert Indigenous presence in Brazilian art. https://www.biennaleofsydney.art/participants/denilson-baniwa/
- **Deniz Tortum** (1) — Filmmaker and media artist. Turkish filmmaker and researcher (MIT Open Documentary Lab alumnus) working across documentary, VR and installation.
- **Diana Domingues** (1) — Media artist and researcher, University of Caxias do Sul. Brazilian pioneer of interactive, robotic and telematic art (1947–2025) who led the ARTECHNO research group at the University of Caxias do Sul. https://digitalartarchive.at/database/artist/15/
- **Diego Galafassi** (1) — Artist and sustainability researcher. Brazilian-born artist and researcher (Stockholm Resilience Centre) working with immersive media and climate.
- **Dominic Wilcox** (1) — Designer and inventor. British designer and artist known for playful inventions and participatory projects. https://www.dominicwilcox.com/
- **Don Allison** (1) — VR researcher, GVU Center, Georgia Tech. Georgia Tech graduate researcher who led the Virtual Reality Gorilla Exhibit with Larry F. Hodges and Zoo Atlanta in the mid-1990s.
- **Double Dagger Studio** (1) — Independent game studio, Seattle. Small studio founded by former Valve designer Matt T. Wood; made Little Kitty, Big City.
- **Dunne & Raby** (1) — Critical design practice (Anthony Dunne, Fiona Raby). Anthony Dunne and Fiona Raby led the RCA Design Interactions programme and defined speculative and critical design. http://dunneandraby.co.uk
- **E-Line Media** (1) — Game developer and publisher. Studio making games with educational and scientific partners, including Never Alone and Beyond Blue. https://elinemedia.com
- **Ed Annunziata** (1) — Game designer, creator of Ecco the Dolphin. Sega designer who conceived Ecco the Dolphin (1992), developed with Novotrade International.
- **Edo Fouilloux** (1) — Creative technologist. Creative director and co-founder of the social music VR platform PatchXR. https://patchxr.com
- **Edwina Portocarrero** (1) — Designer and researcher. Designer and former MIT Media Lab researcher (Responsive Environments) who led ListenTree.
- **Ehud Ahissar** (1) — Neuroscientist, Weizmann Institute of Science. Ehud Ahissar studies active touch in rats and humans.
- **Ehud Sharlin** (1) — Professor of computer science, University of Calgary. Ehud Sharlin leads the uTouch group on human–robot interaction and physical interfaces.
- **Elie Zananiri** (1) — Creative technologist. Creative coder and technical director of Forager, who built its volumetric time-lapse pipeline for mushroom growth.
- **Elisa Giaccardi** (1) — Design researcher, Politecnico di Milano. Design researcher (formerly TU Delft) who pioneered thing-centred design, using data gathered by things themselves.
- **Eliza McNitt** (1) — Director. American director of science-driven VR, including Spheres and Fistful of Stars.
- **Elke Reinhuber** (1) — Media artist and scholar. Media artist and academic (City University of Hong Kong) working on expanded cinema and immersive media.
- **Emblematic Group** (1) — Immersive journalism studio. Studio founded by Nonny de la Peña producing room-scale immersive journalism. https://emblematicgroup.com
- **Emi Tamaki** (1) — HCI researcher and entrepreneur. Researcher who created PossessedHand, an electrical muscle stimulation band that moves the wearer's fingers, and later founded H2L.
- **Emma Davie** (1) — Documentary filmmaker. Scottish documentary director and teacher at Edinburgh College of Art; co-directed Becoming Animal with Peter Mettler.
- **Emma Roberts** (1) — XR producer. Australian producer and co-creator of Gondwana VR. https://gondwanavr.com
- **EpiXR Games** (1) — Independent game studio, Germany. Small German studio behind the Aery series of calm bird-flight games, with a VR edition.
- **Eric Boyd** (1) — Maker, Sensebridge. Maker who co-founded Sensebridge and designed the North Paw, an open-source compass anklet.
- **Eric Chahi** (1) — Game designer. French game designer of Another World and creative director of From Dust at Ubisoft Montpellier.
- **Erin B. Ryan** (1) — Animal welfare researcher, University of British Columbia. Erin Ryan works in Daniel Weary's Animal Welfare Program on how people take the perspective of farm animals.
- **Es Devlin** (1) — Artist and stage designer. British artist known for large-scale sculptures and stage designs that combine light, text and sound. https://esdevlin.com
- **Eugene Birman** (1) — Composer. Composer and assistant professor of music at Hong Kong Baptist University, working on large-scale, data-driven music theatre.
- **Firepunchd Games** (1) — Independent VR game studio, Germany. Small German studio that made the VR physics game Tentacular, published by Devolver Digital. https://www.tentacular.com/
- **Francois Knoetze** (1) — Artist and filmmaker. South African artist who makes wearable sculptures from discarded materials and performs them in films such as Cape Mongo and Core Dump.
- **Frank Schumann** (1) — Cognitive scientist. Cognitive scientist working on sensory augmentation, formerly with Peter König and J. Kevin O'Regan.
- **Frank Swain** (1) — Science writer. Science journalist and hearing-aid user who hacked his hearing aids to hear WiFi, with sound artist Daniel Jones.
- **Freedom Conservation** (1) — Eagle conservation and filming project. French association led by falconer Jacques-Olivier Travers that reintroduces white-tailed eagles and flies trained eagles with cameras over cities and glaciers.
- **Frictional Games** (1) — Game studio. Swedish studio behind the Amnesia series and SOMA. https://frictionalgames.com
- **Frontier Developments** (1) — Game developer, Cambridge. British developer founded by David Braben; made Elite: Dangerous, Planet Zoo and the PS2 game Dog's Life. https://www.frontier.co.uk/
- **Fujiko Nakaya** (1) — Fog sculptor. Japanese artist who has made sculptures of artificial fog since Expo '70, with engineer Thomas Mee.
- **Fumio Mizuno** (1) — Engineer, Tohoku Institute of Technology. Fumio Mizuno built Virtual Chameleon, a wearable that lets each eye look in a different direction.
- **Gamification Group, Tampere University** (1) — Games research group, Tampere University. Juho Hamari's Gamification Group studies games, VR and play, including Oğuz 'Oz' Buruk's work on embodied and more-than-human play.
- **Gattai Games** (1) — Independent game studio, Singapore. Singapore studio that grew out of a student project into the echolocation horror game Stifled. https://gattaigames.com/
- **Geoff Cox** (1) — Scholar of software culture. Professor of art and computational culture (London South Bank University) and co-editor of APRJA.
- **George Monbiot** (1) — Writer and environmental campaigner. British journalist and author of Feral and Regenesis.
- **Gershon Dublon** (1) — Artist and engineer, co-founder of slow immediate. Artist-engineer and MIT Media Lab PhD who builds sensor networks in wetlands and forests; co-founded slow immediate with Xin Liu. https://slowimmediate.com
- **Giant Squid** (1) — Independent game studio. Studio founded by Matt Nava, art director of Journey; made ABZÛ and The Pathless. https://abzugame.com
- **Gowrishankar Ganesh** (1) — Robotics and neuroscience researcher, CNRS-LIRMM. Gowrishankar Ganesh studies human motor control and embodiment of tools and robots.
- **Greg Marshall** (1) — Marine biologist and filmmaker, inventor of Crittercam. Marine biologist who invented the animal-borne Crittercam and developed it at National Geographic from 1986.
- **Guto Nóbrega** (1) — Artist and professor, NANO lab, Federal University of Rio de Janeiro. Brazilian artist and researcher who builds hybrid creatures from living plants and robotic systems and founded the NANO lab (Núcleo de Arte e Novos Organismos) at UFRJ. https://cargocollective.com/gutonobrega
- **Haoran Xie** (1) — HCI researcher, Japan Advanced Institute of Science and Technology. Haoran Xie's lab designs wearable and augmentation devices.
- **Harun Farocki** (1) — Filmmaker and artist (1944–2014). German filmmaker whose essay films and installations examined images produced by and for machines. https://www.harunfarocki.de
- **Haus-Rucker-Co** (1) — Architecture and art group (Laurids Ortner, Günter Zamp Kelp, Klaus Pinter). Viennese group founded in 1967 whose 'Mind Expanding Program' built pneumatic rooms and perception-altering helmets.
- **Heather Barnett** (1) — Artist and researcher working with slime mould. Artist and educator at Central Saint Martins who has collaborated with the slime mould Physarum polycephalum since 2009. https://heatherbarnett.co.uk
- **Hemisphere Games** (1) — Independent game studio, Toronto. Toronto studio founded by Eddy Boxerman; made Osmos. http://www.hemispheregames.com/
- **Herobeat Studios** (1) — Independent game studio, Spain. Spanish studio that made Endling - Extinction is Forever.
- **Hildegard Westerkamp** (1) — Composer and soundwalk artist. German-born composer and member of the World Soundscape Project; pioneer of the soundwalk. https://www.hildegardwesterkamp.ca
- **Himali Singh Soin** (1) — Artist and writer. Indian artist and writer whose films, performances and texts build speculative mythologies around ice, rocks and deep time. https://www.himalisinghsoin.com
- **Hira Nabi** (1) — Artist and filmmaker. Pakistani artist and filmmaker working on labour, ecology and dispossession; 2020 Prince Claus Next Generation laureate.
- **Hiroki (Hill) Kobayashi** (1) — Researcher in human-computer-biosphere interaction, University of Tokyo. Japanese acoustic ecologist and HCI researcher (chair of the World Forum for Acoustic Ecology around 2010) who connects people to remote forests through live sound, as in Tele Echo Tube and the Cyberforest recordings.
- **Ho Tzu Nyen** (1) — Artist and filmmaker. Singaporean artist making video installations and theatre that rework Southeast Asian history and myth; represented Singapore at the Venice Biennale 2011.
- **Holition** (1) — Creative innovation studio. London studio making interactive and immersive digital installations. https://holition.com
- **Hongyu Zhou** (1) — HCI researcher, University of Sydney. Hongyu Zhou studies control of supernumerary limbs with Anusha Withana.
- **House House** (1) — Independent game studio, Melbourne. Four-person Melbourne studio behind Push Me Pull You and Untitled Goose Game. https://goose.game
- **Iffa Nurlatifah** (1) — Digital media artist and researcher, Multimedia University. Iffa Nurlatifah creates VR films from non-human viewpoints.
- **Infuse Studio** (1) — Independent game studio, Adelaide. Small Australian studio behind Spirit of the North and its sequel. https://playspiritofthenorth.com/
- **Institute of Digital Fashion** (1) — Digital fashion collective (Leanne Elliott Young & Catty Tay). London collective making gender-nonconforming avatars, 3D couture and AR/VR experiences.
- **Interactive Architecture Lab** (1) — Research lab at The Bartlett, UCL, led by Ruairi Glynn. Master's programme and lab at The Bartlett working on responsive, robotic and living architecture. http://www.interactivearchitecture.org
- **Interactive Media Foundation** (1) — Berlin non-profit producing interactive and VR documentaries. Non-profit foundation in Berlin that funds and produces interactive storytelling and VR projects, including Inside Tumucumaque with Filmtank and the Museum für Naturkunde Berlin. https://www.interactivemediafoundation.com/
- **Issay Rodriguez** (1) — Visual artist. Filipino artist working across installation, textile and virtual reality, often with research into bees and other more-than-human worlds; exhibited at the 2017 Venice Biennale and Gasworks, London.
- **J. Kevin O'Regan** (1) — Psychologist, sensorimotor theory. Psychologist whose sensorimotor theory holds that perceiving is mastering how sensations change with action.
- **Jaan Aru** (1) — Neuroscientist, University of Tartu. Jaan Aru studies consciousness and learning; with Madis Vasser he ran the 'human octopus' VR experiments.
- **Jae Rhim Lee** (1) — Artist and founder of Coeio. Artist and MIT-trained designer behind the Infinity Burial Project, which trains mushrooms to decompose human bodies. https://coeio.com
- **Jaime Martínez Harms** (1) — Biologist, INIA La Cruz. Chilean biologist at the Instituto de Investigaciones Agropecuarias (INIA) La Cruz who studies pollinator vision and plant–pollinator interactions.
- **Jakob von Uexküll** (1) — Biologist (1864–1944). Jakob von Uexküll founded the study of the Umwelt, the perceptual world specific to each animal.
- **James Bridle** (1) — Artist and writer. Artist and author of New Dark Age and Ways of Being, working on technology, perception and more-than-human intelligence. https://jamesbridle.com
- **Jan Waligórski** (1) — Philosopher of cognition. Jan Waligórski writes on embodiment, VR and the perspectives of other animals.
- **Jascha Sohl-Dickstein** (1) — Machine learning researcher; former neuroscience researcher at UC Berkeley. Jascha Sohl-Dickstein built the Sonic Eye ultrasonic echolocation headset with Michael DeWeese's lab.
- **Jean-Luc Lugrin** (1) — VR researcher, University of Würzburg. Researcher at the HCI group of the University of Würzburg working on avatars and embodiment.
- **Jenna Sutela** (1) — Artist working with living media and machine learning. Finnish artist who works with slime mould, bacteria and neural networks to explore interspecies and machine language. https://jennasutela.com
- **Jennifer Molnar** (1) — Robotics and VR researcher. Jennifer Molnar works on soft robotics and non-anthropomorphic hands with Yigit Mengüç.
- **Jenova Chen** (1) — Game designer. Chinese-born game designer who studied at USC Interactive Media and co-founded thatgamecompany. https://www.jenovachen.com
- **Jeremy Mendes** (1) — Interactive designer. Canadian interactive filmmaker, co-director of Bear 71 for the National Film Board of Canada.
- **Jessica Rath** (1) — Artist working with plants and pollinators. American artist whose sculpture and installation projects work with plant breeders, pollinator scientists and habitat restoration. http://jessicarath.com/
- **John Desnoyers-Stewart** (1) — Artist and HCI researcher. Researcher at Simon Fraser University's iSpace Lab working on social VR and bio-responsive installations.
- **John Luther Adams** (1) — Composer. American composer whose music comes from Alaskan landscape and weather; Pulitzer Prize 2014. https://www.johnlutheradams.net
- **Jonah King** (1) — Artist. American artist working in VR, film and sculpture; assistant professor at Stevens Institute of Technology. https://jonahking.com
- **Jonathon Keats** (1) — Conceptual artist and experimental philosopher. American conceptual artist and writer whose thought experiments address other species, including films for plants and a ballet for honeybees.
- **Jooyoung Oh** (1) — Media artist and researcher (KAIST GSCT). Korean artist (b. 1991) working with games, artificial cognitive models, VR and drones; winner of the IEEE BRAIN award at Ars Electronica 2019. https://k-artist.com/Artist_Posts.php?tab_num=tab4&co_id=1749371641
- **Joren Vandenbroucke** (1) — Animator and VR director (Animal Tank). Belgian director at Animal Tank, known for comic VR and animation. https://www.animaltank.com
- **Joseph Beuys** (1) — Artist, sculptor and performance artist. German artist (1921–1986) whose actions with animals, a dead hare and a live coyote, made animal encounter central to postwar performance art.
- **Julius Neubronner** (1) — Apothecary and inventor of pigeon photography. German apothecary (1852–1932) and amateur photography pioneer who patented miniature cameras carried by homing pigeons in 1908.
- **Jörg Courtial** (1) — VR director (Faber Courtial). German director and co-founder of the digital studio Faber Courtial, known for VR and dome films on space and evolution. https://www.fabercourtial.de
- **K. Carrie Armel** (1) — Researcher, cognitive neuroscience. Researcher who, with V. S. Ramachandran, showed that people can project touch onto a table.
- **Kate Crawford** (1) — Scholar of AI and society. Researcher and author of Atlas of AI who studies the politics of machine learning datasets.
- **Katerina Kamprani** (1) — Architect and designer. Greek designer behind The Uncomfortable, a series of deliberately useless everyday objects. https://www.theuncomfortable.com
- **Keiken** (1) — Artist collective working with game engines, haptics and world-building. Founded in 2015 by Tanya Cruz, Hana Omori and Isabel Ramos; Keiken (Japanese for 'experience') builds game worlds, AR filters and wearable haptic 'wombs' that imagine posthuman bodies and forms of empathy. https://keiken.cloud/
- **Keisuke Suzuki** (1) — Researcher, consciousness science and VR. Researcher (Sackler Centre for Consciousness Science, University of Sussex) who builds VR platforms to study perception.
- **Keita Higuchi** (1) — HCI researcher. Researcher who, in Jun Rekimoto's lab at the University of Tokyo, built Flying Head, a drone that follows the pilot's head.
- **Ken Goldberg** (1) — Artist and roboticist, UC Berkeley. Roboticist and artist who made early internet telerobotic artworks, including the Telegarden. https://goldberg.berkeley.edu
- **Ken Thaiday Snr** (1) — Torres Strait Islander artist and dancer. Meriam artist from Erub (Darnley Island) who makes kinetic dance headdresses of the hammerhead shark (beizam) and co-founded the Darnley Island Dance Troupe (1987).
- **Kenichi Okada** (1) — Designer. Japanese designer, graduate of the Royal College of Art Design Interactions programme, who co-created Animal Superpowers with Chris Woebken.
- **Kevin Ponto** (1) — VR researcher, Wisconsin Institute for Discovery, University of Wisconsin–Madison. Kevin Ponto works on virtual environments for science and learning, with David Gagnon's Field Day Lab.
- **Kevin Warwick** (1) — Engineer, cybernetics researcher. British engineer who implanted chips and a nerve electrode array in his own body in Project Cyborg (1998, 2002).
- **Kingsley Ng** (1) — Visual artist. Hong Kong artist working with light, site and sound; represented Hong Kong at the Venice Biennale 2026 with Angel Hui.
- **Knowbotic Research** (1) — Media art group (Yvonne Wilhelm, Christian Hübler, Alexander Tuchacek). Artist group founded in 1991 that builds interfaces to data networks and remote environments.
- **Krillbite Studio** (1) — Independent game studio, Norway. Norwegian studio founded by students; made The Plan, Among the Sleep and Mosaic. http://krillbite.com/
- **Kuang-Yi Ku** (1) — Bio-artist, social designer and dentist. Taiwanese bio-artist and practising dentist with degrees from Design Academy Eindhoven; known for the Tiger Penis Project and Bat Night Market. https://www.kukuangyi.com/
- **Kyle McDonald** (1) — Artist working with code. Media artist who works with computer vision and machine learning, often in public space. https://kylemcdonald.net
- **Lai Guan-yuan** (1) — XR director. Taiwanese director who made the mixed-reality literary documentary Black Wings for Taiwan's Cultural Kuroshio XR programme.
- **Larry F. Hodges** (1) — VR researcher, Georgia Tech, later Clemson University. Virtual-environments researcher known for VR exposure therapy and presence studies.
- **Laurie Anderson** (1) — Artist, composer and performer. American multimedia artist who has made several VR works with Hsin-Chien Huang, including Chalkroom and To the Moon. https://laurieanderson.com
- **Lawrence Lek** (1) — Simulation artist and filmmaker. Artist of Malaysian Chinese descent who builds game-engine films and open-world simulations about AI, Sinofuturism and machine consciousness. https://lawrencelek.com
- **Leah Barclay** (1) — Sound artist and acoustic ecologist. Australian composer and researcher working with hydrophones in rivers, reefs and biosphere reserves. https://leahbarclay.com
- **Leanne Allison** (1) — Filmmaker. Canadian wildlife filmmaker who co-directed the NFB interactive documentary Bear 71.
- **Lena Thiele** (1) — Interactive media director. German director of interactive documentaries; co-directed Myriad with Sebastian Baurmann and Dirk Hoffmann for the Interactive Media Foundation. https://www.interactivemedia-foundation.com
- **Leslie Kay** (1) — Engineer, inventor of ultrasonic mobility aids (1922–2020). Leslie Kay designed the Sonic Torch and binaural Sonicguide, air-sonar aids for blind people, from the 1960s.
- **Lienzo** (1) — Independent game studio. Mexican independent game studio known for Mulaka (2018), an action-adventure game built on the mythology of the Rarámuri (Tarahumara) people of the Sierra Tarahumara.
- **Llyr ap Cenydd** (1) — Developer and researcher in procedural animal animation. Welsh developer whose PhD on procedural animation of marine animals grew into the VR aquarium Ocean Rift. https://ocean-rift.com
- **Lone Koefoed Hansen** (1) — Interaction design researcher. Associate professor at Aarhus University working on critical and aesthetic interaction design.
- **Lore Thaler** (1) — Psychologist, Durham University. Lore Thaler studies human click-based echolocation and teaches it to blind and sighted people.
- **Louis-Philippe Demers** (1) — Artist, robotic performance. Artist who builds large robotic installations and performances, often with Bill Vorn.
- **Lucien Castaing-Taylor** (1) — Filmmaker and anthropologist, Harvard Sensory Ethnography Lab. Directs the Sensory Ethnography Lab at Harvard; films include Sweetgrass and Leviathan.
- **Luke Jerram** (1) — Artist. British artist known for touring works such as Museum of the Moon and Gaia. https://www.lukejerram.com
- **Luma Octo** (1) — Immersive studio (Max Sacker & Arite Szadkowski). German studio of filmmakers Max Sacker and Arite Szadkowski making interactive, musical immersive cinema.
- **Lygia Clark** (1) — Artist. Brazilian Neo-Concrete artist (1920–1988) who moved from sculpture to 'relational objects' and sensorial masks worn by participants.
- **MICROBIhOME (University of Salford)** (1) — Science-art microbiome project. University of Salford project led by microbiologist Chloe James with artist Paul Miller, building immersive installations about the human microbiome. https://hub.salford.ac.uk/microbihome/
- **Manekoware** (1) — Independent game studio of Chris Chung. One-person studio of Chris Chung, who made the first-person cat game Catlateral Damage, later co-developed with Fire Hose Games. http://www.catlateraldamage.com/
- **Manel De Aguas** (1) — Cyborg artist and musician. Artist who wears head fins that sense air pressure, humidity and temperature and turn the weather into sound.
- **Marc Erich Latoschik** (1) — Professor of HCI, University of Würzburg. HCI professor who leads research on avatars, embodiment and social VR.
- **Marcel·lí Antúnez Roca** (1) — Performance artist. Catalan artist, co-founder of La Fura dels Baus, known for mechatronic performances with exoskeletons and body interfaces. https://www.marceliantunez.com
- **Marco Donnarumma** (1) — Performance artist and researcher. Italian artist who builds performances from muscle sounds, biosignals, prosthetics and machine learning. https://marcodonnarumma.com
- **Marcus Maeder** (1) — Sound artist and acoustic ecologist. Swiss sound artist and researcher at ZHdK and ETH who records and sonifies the inner sounds of trees and soils. https://blog.zhdk.ch/marcusmaeder/
- **Mark Thompson** (1) — Artist and beekeeper. American artist who has worked with honeybees since the 1970s in performances and installations.
- **Martin Kocur** (1) — HCI researcher, University of Applied Sciences Upper Austria. Martin Kocur studies avatar embodiment and its effects on perception and performance.
- **Maryam Alimardani** (1) — Researcher, brain-computer interfaces and robot embodiment. Researcher who, working with Ishiguro's group, used brain-computer interfaces to let people move android hands by thought.
- **Matthew Botvinick** (1) — Cognitive neuroscientist, Princeton / Google DeepMind. Matthew Botvinick, with Jonathan Cohen, first described the rubber hand illusion in 1998.
- **Matthew R. Longo** (1) — Professor of psychology, Birkbeck, University of London. Matthew Longo studies body representation, touch and illusions of the body.
- **Max Rheiner** (1) — Interaction designer, creator of Birdly. Swiss artist and former lecturer in Interaction Design at the Zurich University of the Arts (ZHdK), where he built the first Birdly prototype in 2013 before co-founding SOMNIACS.
- **Maya Mouawad** (1) — Artist and designer. Franco-Lebanese designer of interactive installations, co-author of Peupler with composer Cyril Laurier.
- **Mel Slater** (1) — VR researcher, University of Barcelona (Event Lab); formerly UCL. Mel Slater pioneered research on presence and virtual body ownership, from body swaps to extended avatars.
- **Michael Girard** (1) — Computer animator, co-creator of Menagerie. Animator and researcher known for procedural legged-locomotion animation; co-founded Unreal Pictures with Susan Amkraut.
- **Michael Pinsky** (1) — Artist. British artist making public works about environment and urban life. https://www.michaelpinsky.com
- **Michelle Chang** (1) — Design researcher. Researcher who with Lining Yao's Morphing Matter Lab mapped the design space of human-plant interaction.
- **Mie C. S. Egeberg** (1) — Medialogy researcher, Aalborg University. Mie Egeberg and colleagues, with Martin Kraus, studied ownership of virtual wings.
- **Milica Zec** (1) — Film director and VR storyteller. Serbian-born director and editor who co-founded the VR studio New Reality Co. with Winslow Porter; directed Giant and Tree. https://www.treeofficial.com
- **Miri Chekhanovich** (1) — Artist and filmmaker. Israeli-Canadian visual artist; co-created Plastisapiens with Édith Jorisch (NFB and DPT).
- **Momoko Seto** (1) — Filmmaker and artist. Japanese-born, Paris-based filmmaker whose PLANET series films real micro-organisms, fungi and insects in macro time-lapse as alien worlds.
- **Monkeystack** (1) — Digital production studio. Australian studio that produced Thin Ice VR with explorer Tim Jarvis. https://www.thinicevr.com
- **Mooneye Studios** (1) — Independent game studio, Hamburg. German studio that made Lost Ember, a game about a wolf who can inhabit other animals. https://www.lostember.com
- **Myron Krueger** (1) — Computer artist, pioneer of 'artificial reality'. Built responsive environments from 1969 onward (Glowflow, Metaplay, Psychic Space) and coined 'artificial reality'.
- **Nam June Paik** (1) — Video artist. Korean-American founder of video art (1932–2006), who combined televisions with plants, fish and bodies.
- **Natalie Jeremijenko** (1) — Artist and engineer, Environmental Health Clinic, NYU. Artist-engineer whose Environmental Health Clinic prescribes actions with other species; works include Feral Robotic Dogs, OOZ and Tree Logic.
- **Natasha Tontey** (1) — Artist and filmmaker. Indonesian artist from Minahasa, North Sulawesi, who makes campy speculative films and installations about Indigenous ritual, pests and nonhuman kin.
- **Ndemic Creations** (1) — Game studio. Studio founded by James Vaughan, creator of the pathogen strategy game Plague Inc. https://www.ndemiccreations.com
- **Neil Harbisson** (1) — Cyborg artist. Artist born with achromatopsia who hears colour through an antenna implanted in his skull, and co-founder of the Cyborg Foundation. https://www.cyborgarts.com
- **Neven A. M. ElSayed** (1) — AR researcher, University of South Australia. Neven ElSayed works on situated visualization in augmented reality.
- **Nina Barbier** (1) — Documentary filmmaker. French director of science documentaries, including work with the Muséum national d'Histoire naturelle.
- **Nintendo** (1) — Game company, Kyoto. Japanese game company; Mole Mania was produced by Shigeru Miyamoto. https://www.nintendo.com/
- **No Code** (1) — Game studio. Scottish studio known for Stories Untold and Observation.
- **Old B1ood** (1) — Independent game studio. Small studio that made the fish survival sandbox Feed and Grow: Fish. http://www.feedandgrow.net
- **Oleg Kulik** (1) — Performance artist known as the man-dog. Ukrainian-born performance artist, sculptor and curator best known for performances in the 1990s in which he acted as a dog.
- **Ory Laboratory** (1) — Robotics company founded by Kentaro Yoshifuji. Company behind the OriHime avatar robots, which let people who cannot leave home be present, speak and work through a robot body. https://orylab.com
- **Owen Harris** (1) — VR designer. Designer who created DEEP, a breath-controlled VR experience, with Niki Smit and Monobanda Play. https://www.exploredeep.com
- **Paul Bach-y-Rita** (1) — Neuroscientist (1934–2006). Neuroscientist who pioneered sensory substitution, from the 1969 tactile vision chair to the tongue-based BrainPort.
- **Pedro Lopes** (1) — HCI researcher, University of Chicago. Researcher who uses electrical muscle stimulation to let computers and objects act on the human body. https://lab.plopes.org
- **Perttu Hämäläinen** (1) — Professor of computer games, Aalto University. Perttu Hämäläinen studies movement-based games, exergames and simulated characters.
- **Peter Meijer** (1) — Engineer, inventor of The vOICe. Dutch engineer at Philips Research who developed The vOICe, a visual-to-auditory sensory-substitution system, from 1991. https://www.seeingwithsound.com
- **Peter Mettler** (1) — Filmmaker and cinematographer. Canadian-Swiss filmmaker known for Gambling, Gods and LSD and The End of Time, films about perception and time.
- **Phobia Game Studio** (1) — Independent game studio, Poland. Polish studio that made the reverse-horror game Carrion, published by Devolver Digital. https://www.devolverdigital.com/games/carrion
- **Pierre Huyghe** (1) — Artist building living, changing exhibitions. French artist known for exhibitions that include living organisms, from Zoodram aquariums to the bee-headed statue of Untilled and the fly-filled UUmwelt.
- **Pierre Zandrowicz** (1) — VR director (Atlas V). French director and co-founder of the immersive studio Atlas V.
- **Pingting Chen** (1) — Interaction design researcher. Pingting Chen and colleagues designed ZooWear, animal-ear wearables for zoo visitors.
- **Polymorf** (1) — Interdisciplinary artist collective for multisensory XR. Dutch collective led by Marcel van Brakel that builds multisensory VR installations with haptic suits, soft robotics, scent and food. https://polymorf.nl
- **Rachael Rakena** (1) — Māori video artist. Kāi Tahu and Ngāpuhi artist working with video, water and digital Māori identity; associate professor at Massey University.
- **Rachel Mayeri** (1) — Media artist working with primates. American artist and professor at Harvey Mudd College whose videos and installations explore the science of animal behaviour, including films made for chimpanzee audiences. https://rachelmayeri.com/
- **Rachel Strickland** (1) — Architect and video artist. American architect, filmmaker and interaction designer who co-directed Placeholder and shot its landscapes in Banff.
- **Random International** (1) — Art collective. Collective founded by Hannes Koch and Florian Ortkrass, making works about behaviour and perception. https://www.random-international.com
- **Ready At Dawn** (1) — Game studio. Game studio known for the VR games Lone Echo and Echo VR.
- **Rebecca Allen** (1) — Media artist and professor, UCLA Design Media Arts. Pioneer of computer animation and artificial-life art since the 1970s; founding chair of UCLA Design Media Arts. http://www.rebeccaallen.com
- **Regine Rapp** (1) — Art historian and curator, Art Laboratory Berlin. Co-director of Art Laboratory Berlin, which curated the Nonhuman Networks and Mind the Fungi programmes. https://artlaboratory-berlin.org
- **Revolutionary Games Studio** (1) — Volunteer open-source game project. International volunteer community developing Thrive, a free, open-source evolution game guided by biology. https://revolutionarygamesstudio.com/
- **Richard Vijgen** (1) — Information designer. Dutch designer who makes data visualisations of invisible infrastructures. https://www.richardvijgen.nl
- **Rob Spence** (1) — Filmmaker. Canadian filmmaker who replaced his lost eye with a prosthetic containing a wireless video camera.
- **Robert Charles Johnson** (1) — Artist and performance maker. UK artist working across performance, food and speculative design; co-created Bat Night Market with Kuang-Yi Ku.
- **Roger Payne** (1) — Biologist. American biologist (1935–2023) who, with Scott McVay, discovered that humpback whales sing.
- **Ron Wakkary** (1) — Design researcher, Simon Fraser University. Design researcher and author of Things We Could Design, who leads the Everyday Design Studio.
- **Ronny Andrade** (1) — Accessibility and HCI researcher, University of Melbourne. Ronny Andrade works with expert blind echolocators to design echolocation-based virtual environments.
- **Ryuichi Sakamoto** (1) — Composer. Composer and founding member of Yellow Magic Orchestra (1952–2023); in later years made installations about sound, environment and nature.
- **Sam Easterson** (1) — Video artist filming from animals' points of view. American video artist trained in fine art and landscape architecture who since 1998 has attached small cameras to animals and plants to build a library of nonhuman points of view.
- **Samira Poudratchi** (1) — Game researcher, Tabriz Islamic Art University. Samira Poudratchi designs audio games for navigation.
- **Samurai Punk** (1) — Independent game studio, Melbourne. Melbourne studio that made Screencheat and the bird-flight game Feather. https://samuraipunk.com/
- **Sang-won Leigh** (1) — Assistant professor, Georgia Tech; formerly MIT Media Lab Fluid Interfaces. Sang-won Leigh designs body-extending robots and wrote on human–machine body plasticity with Pattie Maes.
- **Sarah Silverblatt-Buser** (1) — Choreographer and XR artist. American choreographer and filmmaker making movement-based VR and dance films.
- **Sbug Games** (1) — Independent game studio. Small studio that made the spider platformer Webbed and its spin-off Isopod.
- **ScanLAB Projects** (1) — 3D scanning studio (Matthew Shaw & William Trossell). London studio using LiDAR scanning for architecture, film and art. https://scanlabprojects.co.uk
- **Scenocosme** (1) — Interactive art duo (Grégory Lasserre & Anaïs met den Ancxt). French duo who since 2007 have made installations in which living plants sense human touch and answer with sound. http://www.scenocosme.com
- **Scott Fisher** (1) — VR pioneer, NASA Ames VIEW lab and Telepresence Research. Led the NASA Ames Virtual Interface Environment Workstation in the 1980s and later founded Telepresence Research and the USC Mobile and Environmental Media Lab.
- **Seungwoo Je** (1) — HCI researcher, Southern University of Science and Technology. Seungwoo Je leads the Immersive Design Group, working on haptic devices for VR.
- **Shizuko Hiryu** (1) — Bat biologist, Doshisha University. Shizuko Hiryu studies bat echolocation and flight; her lab also builds echolocation training for humans.
- **Shuai Zou** (1) — Media artist and researcher, HKUST (Guangzhou). Shuai Zou and colleagues in Zeyu Wang's lab made The Dream of Zhuang Zhou.
- **Shuichi Nishio** (1) — Researcher, ATR Hiroshi Ishiguro Laboratories. Researcher at ATR who led the studies on body ownership transfer to teleoperated androids.
- **Shuran Fan** (1) — Design researcher. Shuran Fan designs more-than-human wearables for everyday nature.
- **Siqi Yu** (1) — Games researcher, HKUST (Guangzhou). Siqi Yu, with Shuai Liu, Yiqing Tian and Mar Canet Sola, analysed first-person VR games with animal avatars.
- **Siyeon Kim** (1) — XR animation director, Studio Metapo. Korean director of the XR animation My Name is O90, produced by Studio Metapo and the Korean Academy of Film Arts. http://studiometapo.com
- **Soroosh Mashal** (1) — Game and VR researcher, University of Passau. Soroosh Mashal studies how people imagine wings and flying movements in VR.
- **Sputniko!** (1) — Artist working in speculative and critical design. British-Japanese artist (Hiromi Ozaki) whose videos and devices, first made at the Royal College of Art, question how emerging technologies shape bodies, gender and society; later an associate professor at the MIT Media Lab and Tokyo University of the Arts. https://sputniko.com/
- **Stefania Serafin** (1) — Professor of sonic interaction design, Aalborg University Copenhagen. Stefania Serafin leads the Multisensory Experience Lab, working on sound, haptics and embodiment in VR.
- **Stephan Harding** (1) — Ecologist and educator. Ecologist and Gaia theorist at Schumacher College who designed the Deep Time Walk. https://www.deeptimewalk.org
- **Steye Hallema** (1) — Artist, founder of The Smartphone Orchestra. Dutch artist making collective experiences on audiences' own phones (The Smartphone Orchestra, The Imaginary Friend). https://smartphoneorchestra.com
- **Sumo Digital** (1) — Game developer, Sheffield. Large British developer; its internal game jam produced Snake Pass. https://www.sumo-digital.com/
- **Susan Amkraut** (1) — Computer animator and virtual-environment artist. Computer animation pioneer who, with Michael Girard, built behavioural animation systems for flocks and walking creatures in the late 1980s and early 1990s.
- **Susana Soares** (1) — Designer, London South Bank University. Portuguese designer and researcher, graduate of the RCA Design Interactions programme, working on biology-driven speculative design.
- **Susumu Tachi** (1) — Roboticist, pioneer of telexistence. Professor emeritus at the University of Tokyo who proposed telexistence in 1980 and led the TELESAR series of surrogate robots. https://tachilab.org
- **Tae Yeun Kim** (1) — Media artist and VR director. Korean artist who made the interactive VR work Helpless (2021) with PPPLab and VR Crew, shown in the Beyond Science strand of BIFAN's Beyond Reality programme.
- **Taiwoo Park** (1) — HCI researcher, Michigan State University. Taiwoo Park's lab designed JediFlight, a wing-based VR flying game.
- **Takuji Narumi** (1) — Associate professor, University of Tokyo. Takuji Narumi studies how virtual bodies and multisensory cues change perception and behaviour.
- **Tamar Makin** (1) — Professor of cognitive neuroscience, University of Cambridge (Plasticity Lab). Tamar Makin studies how the brain represents the body, prosthetics and augmentation.
- **Tangjun Qu** (1) — HCI researcher, Shandong University. Tangjun Qu and colleagues study the Proteus effect of non-human avatars in VR.
- **Temsüyanger Longkumer** (1) — Artist. Naga-born artist working between painting, sculpture and VR; his VR piece home was filmed with a bee colony in Gaili, Nagaland. http://www.temsuyanger.com
- **Tender Claws** (1) — Interactive and VR studio. Studio founded by Samantha Gorman and Danny Cannizzaro, known for Virtual Virtual Reality, The Under Presents and Face Jumping. https://tenderclaws.com/
- **The Deep End Games** (1) — Independent game studio founded by former BioShock developers. Studio founded by Bill and Amanda Gardner and other former Irrational Games staff; made Perception. https://thedeependgames.com/
- **The Living** (1) — Architecture studio (David Benjamin, Soo-in Yang). New York architecture studio founded by David Benjamin and Soo-in Yang, working with living materials and environmental sensing.
- **The Swan Collective** (1) — Art collective (Felix Kraus). Collective founded by Felix Kraus making VR and installation art.
- **Theresa Schubert** (1) — Artist working with fungi and simulation. German artist whose installations treat fungi and other organisms as co-creators; part of the Mind the Fungi project at TU Berlin. https://www.theresaschubert.com
- **Thomas Nagel** (1) — Philosopher, New York University. Thomas Nagel is a philosopher of mind and ethics.
- **Thomas Thwaites** (1) — Designer, author of GoatMan. British designer known for The Toaster Project and GoatMan, speculative projects that test technology with his own body; shared the 2016 Ig Nobel Prize in Biology with Charles Foster. https://www.thomasthwaites.com/
- **Tom Froese** (1) — Cognitive scientist, Okinawa Institute of Science and Technology. Researcher in enactive cognitive science and artificial life who builds minimal sensory-substitution devices to study perception.
- **Tom Mustill** (1) — Filmmaker, naturalist and author. Wildlife filmmaker and author of How to Speak Whale (2022), who works with scientists using machine learning to study animal communication. https://www.tommustill.com
- **Tom White** (1) — Artist and lecturer. Artist who makes abstract prints designed to be recognised by neural networks. https://drib.net
- **Tony J. Prescott** (1) — Professor of cognitive robotics, University of Sheffield. Tony Prescott builds whiskered robots and biomimetic touch based on rodents.
- **Tower Five** (1) — Game studio, France. French studio that made Empire of the Ants for publisher Microids. https://www.microids.com/empire-of-the-ants/
- **Treta Studios** (1) — VR game studio, India. Indian VR studio that made Be A Chameleon for Meta Quest and Steam.
- **Tripwire Interactive** (1) — Game developer and publisher, Georgia (USA). Developer and publisher known for Killing Floor and Maneater. https://tripwireinteractive.com/
- **Ubisoft Montreal** (1) — Game studio of Ubisoft, Montreal. Large Ubisoft studio known for Assassin's Creed and Far Cry; its Fun House team made the early VR title Eagle Flight. https://montreal.ubisoft.com/
- **United Visual Artists** (1) — London art studio working with light and sound. London studio founded by Matt Clark in 2003, making installations with light, sound and code. https://www.uva.co.uk/
- **Untame** (1) — Independent game studio, Israel. Small studio of Itay Keren and Julia Keren-Detar; made Mushroom 11. http://mushroom11.com
- **Ursula Biemann** (1) — Artist and theorist. Swiss video artist working on geology, climate and Indigenous knowledge. https://www.geobodies.org
- **Uýra Sodoma** (1) — Indigenous travesti performance artist and biologist. Born in Santarém, Pará (1991), trained in biology and ecology; since 2016 transforms into Uýra, 'the tree that walks', using leaves, bark, seeds and pigments. PIPA Prize 2022.
- **V. S. Ramachandran** (1) — Neuroscientist, UC San Diego. Neuroscientist known for the mirror box and studies of phantom limbs and body image.
- **VARSAV Game Studios** (1) — Game studio, Warsaw. Polish studio that made Bee Simulator and its sequel Bee Simulator: The Hive. https://beesimulator.com/
- **Vera Vasas** (1) — Sensory ecologist, Queen Mary University of London / George Mason University. Vera Vasas works with Daniel Hanley on cameras and software that record colour as animals see it.
- **Victor Mateevitsi** (1) — Researcher, Electronic Visualization Laboratory, University of Illinois Chicago. Computer scientist who built SpiderSense, a suit that lets wearers feel nearby objects through the skin.
- **Videocult** (1) — Independent game studio. Studio of Joar Jakobsson and James Therrien (James Primate); made Rain World.
- **Vision3** (1) — Immersive studio. London studio founded by Chris Parks; made Drop in the Ocean with Conservation International.
- **Visiontrick Media** (1) — Game studio. Swedish independent studio (director Rui Guerreiro) behind the VR games Pan-Pan and Mare. https://www.visiontrick.com
- **Viviana Álvarez Chomón** (1) — Designer and media arts researcher. Chilean designer and researcher in media arts who develops science-art VR experiences with biologists.
- **Véréna Paravel** (1) — Filmmaker and anthropologist, Harvard Sensory Ethnography Lab. French-Swiss artist and anthropologist working in the Sensory Ethnography Lab; co-director of Leviathan, Caniba and De Humani Corporis Fabrica.
- **Wevr** (1) — Virtual reality studio. Los Angeles VR studio behind theBlu series and the Transport platform. https://wevr.com
- **Within** (1) — VR studio (Chris Milk & Aaron Koblin). Studio founded by Chris Milk and Aaron Koblin, early producer of cinematic and social VR. https://www.with.in
- **Wolfgang Buttress** (1) — Artist. British artist who made The Hive for the UK Pavilion at Expo Milano 2015 with physicist Martin Bencsik. https://www.wolfgangbuttress.com
- **Xin Liu** (1) — Artist and engineer, co-founder of slow immediate. Artist-engineer and MIT Media Lab alumna whose studio slow immediate (with Gershon Dublon) makes works on perception, the body and the environment. https://slowimmediate.com
- **Xinmiao Lan** (1) — Communication researcher, University of Amsterdam. Xinmiao Lan studies avatars and the Proteus effect with Zeph van Berlo.
- **Xu Bing** (1) — Artist. Chinese artist known for Book from the Sky; his first feature film, Dragonfly Eyes (2017), is edited entirely from public surveillance footage. https://www.xubing.com
- **YCAM InterLab** (1) — R&D lab of the Yamaguchi Center for Arts and Media. In-house research and development team of YCAM that co-produces media art, bio-art and sensing projects. https://www.ycam.jp
- **Yao Xu** (1) — HCI researcher. Yao Xu and colleagues built iStrayPaws, a VR perspective-taking system about stray animals.
- **Yedan Qian** (1) — Interaction designer. Designer and MIT Media Lab alumna who co-created TreeSense with Xin Liu.
- **Yifu Zhou** (1) — VFX supervisor and VR director, Digital Domain. Tsinghua and USC-trained VFX supervisor who led Digital Domain's first Greater China original VR animation, Micro Giants (2017).
- **Yiou Wang** (1) — Media artist, founder of Trans Species Collective. Media artist working on trans-species empathy through sound and VR; leads the Trans Species Collective that made BATOPIA with HCI researcher Yujie Wang. https://yiouwang.org/
- **Yoshifumi Kitamura** (1) — Professor, Tohoku University RIEC. HCI researcher whose group at Tohoku University works on spatial interfaces, including drone piloting in augmented reality.
- **Young Horses** (1) — Independent game studio, Chicago. Studio formed by DePaul University students after their student game Octodad (2010). https://store.steampowered.com/app/224480/
- **Yu Jiang** (1) — HCI researcher, Tsinghua University. Yu Jiang works with Yukang Yan and David Lindlbauer on controlling non-humanoid avatars.
- **Yu-Lun Hsu** (1) — HCI researcher, National Taiwan University. Yu-Lun Hsu and colleagues designed AnimalSense, a VR game about the senses of animals.
- **Yukinori Yanagi** (1) — Artist known for ant-farm flag works. Japanese artist whose Ant Farm series, shown at the 1993 Venice Biennale, lets ant colonies erode national flags made of sand. https://yanagistudio.net/
- **Ze Gao** (1) — Media art researcher. Researcher in computational media art and digital ecology.
- **Zheng Bo** (1) — Artist working with plants and eco-queer practice. Beijing-born, Hong Kong-based artist (b. 1974) who makes films, drawings and body practices with plants, including Pteridophilia, The Political Life of Plants and Wanwu Practice. http://zhengbo.org/
- **Zoom Inc.** (1) — Game developer, Japan. Japanese developer that made Mister Mosquito (Ka) for PlayStation 2.
- **eduweb** (1) — Educational game developer, Minnesota. Educational media company that created WolfQuest with the Minnesota Zoo, first funded by the US National Science Foundation. http://www.wolfquest.org
- **onformative** (1) — Studio for digital art and design. Berlin studio founded by Cedric Kiefer and Julia Laub that makes generative, data-driven installations about nature and perception. https://onformative.com
- **Édith Jorisch** (1) — Filmmaker. Canadian filmmaker and visual artist, co-creator of Plastisapiens.
- **Özge Samancı** (1) — Media artist and graphic novelist, Northwestern University. Turkish-born media artist and author of the graphic memoirs Dare to Disappoint and Evil Eyes Sea; she makes interactive installations with brain-computer interfaces and teaches at Northwestern University. https://ozgesamanci.com
